Method: AutoSchema框架采用无需训练的方法，通过四个关键步骤实现实时模式接地：1) 检查实时模式；2) 将问题中的实体名映射到图谱标识符；3) 探索关系路径；4) 在迭代查询构建过程中发现资源间可能的连接。

Result: 在Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA、Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA、Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA（BioASQ Task B）和化学知识图谱迁移实验中，AutoSchema相比TogoMCP在生物医学KGQA任务中提高了平均事实准确率，在BioASQ纵向评估中获得一致增益，减少了迭代预算耗尽率，平均使用更少的工具调用。迁移实验初步证明实时模式接地能支持不规则和未见过的图谱。

Conclusion: 实时模式接地是构建知识图谱问答代理的有效替代方案，无需预先创建和维护模式文件即可支持语言模型代理查询知识图谱，且能泛化到未见过的图谱资源，为知识图谱与语言模型代理的集成提供了一种更灵活、可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Life science knowledge graphs make large collections of structured data available through SPARQL, but each resource uses its own schema, identifiers, and links. TogoMCP helps language model agents query these resources by providing curated Metadata Interoperability Exchange files. Creating and maintaining these files still requires language model assisted drafting, validation, and manual review. We study \emph{live schema grounding}, where an agent obtains the schema evidence needed for a question directly from the current endpoints. We present \textsc{autoschema}, a general framework for live schema grounding that requires no training. It inspects live schemas, maps entity names in a question to graph identifiers, explores relation paths, and finds possible connections between resources during iterative query construction. We use TogoMCP as our main comparison framework. We evaluate \textsc{autoschema} on Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA, Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA, Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA over BioASQ Task B, and Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer to a previously undocumented RDF graph. \textsc{autoschema} improves mean factoid accuracy over TogoMCP in the biomedical KGQA tasks and gives consistent gains in the longitudinal BioASQ evaluation. It also reduces iteration budget exhaustion and uses fewer tool calls on average in the core evaluation. The transfer study gives preliminary evidence that live schema grounding can support irregular and previously unseen graphs without first creating a curated schema file.

</details>


### [158] [Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14264)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Vamshi Bonagiri,Raul Astudillo,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOBO-Merge，一个与合并算子无关的框架，利用多目标贝叶斯优化在有限评估预算下近似Pareto前沿，用于模型合并中的参数选择问题。在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B的双模型与三模型合并设置中，MOBO-Merge在12项对比中11项获得更高平均超体积，尤其在TIES、块状合并和三目标搜索中优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并虽然计算高效，但合并参数的选择非常困难：下游评估代价高昂、梯度不可用、源模型能力可能相互冲突。现有方法缺乏系统化的参数搜索策略。

Method: 将合并参数选择建模为黑盒多目标优化问题，采用多目标贝叶斯优化来逼近Pareto前沿。框架与具体合并算子无关，可适配Linear、SLERP、TIES和块状合并等多种算子，并在有限评估预算下工作。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上，使用Linear、SLERP、TIES和块状合并算子在指令-数学（两模型）和指令-数学-代码（三模型）设置中进行评估。MOBO-Merge在12项对比中有11项获得比随机搜索更高的平均超体积。提升在一维Linear插值中较小，但在TIES、块状合并和三目标搜索中显著更大。没有统一最优的合并算子：TIES在四个家族-设置组合中领先三个，Block-Linear 4x在Llama三模型合并中最强。

Conclusion: 多目标贝叶斯优化作为表达能力更强的合并参数化的搜索层具有重要价值，MOBO-Merge为模型合并中的参数选择提供了系统化的黑盒优化方案。

Abstract: Model merging combines trained models directly in weight space, offering a compute-efficient alternative to additional fine-tuning. Selecting merge parameters is nevertheless difficult because downstream evaluations are expensive, gradients are unavailable, and source capabilities can conflict. We formulate merge-parameter selection as a black-box multi-objective optimization problem and introduce MOBO-Merge, a merge-operator agnostic framework that uses multi-objective Bayesian optimization to approximate the Pareto front under a limited evaluation budget. We evaluate Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B in two-model instruction-math and three-model instruction-math-code settings using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and block-wise merge operators. On held-out benchmark partitions, MOBO-Merge obtains higher mean hypervolume than random search in 11 of 12 reported comparisons. The gain is small for one-dimensional Linear interpolation but substantially larger for several TIES, block-wise, and three-objective searches. No merge operator is uniformly best: TIES leads in three of four family-setting combinations, whereas Block-Linear 4x is strongest for the Llama three-model merge. These results show that multi-objective Bayesian optimization is valuable as a search layer for expressive merge parameterizations.

</details>


### [159] [Convex losses and their applications to SVM, SVR, and Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288)
*Filippo Portera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多种新的凸损失函数用于SVM和神经网络的二分类任务，利用模式相关性（pattern correlations）构建损失函数，通过粒子群优化算法求解原问题形式的SVM，并采用嵌套交叉验证评估性能，初步结果表明新损失函数并未显著提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准损失函数（如hinge loss）在分类任务中未充分利用训练样本之间的模式相关性信息，作者希望探索将样本间相关性纳入损失函数设计，以期提升模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了多种新的凸损失函数作为标准损失的推广；由于对偶形式存在实际限制，改用SVM原问题形式并结合粒子群优化（PSO）算法求解；同时将这些损失应用于神经网络；采用嵌套交叉验证（Nested Cross-Validation）评估各损失函数的泛化性能。

Result: 在多个小规模数据集上的实验表明，使用新损失函数与不使用时的泛化度量结果相同，即新损失函数并未带来显著的泛化性能提升；但理论上证明了所提损失是标准损失的推广。

Conclusion: 尽管在理论上将模式相关性融入损失函数有望增强某些数据集的泛化能力，但初步实验结果显示这些新损失函数对泛化性能没有实质性改善，属于一项探索性研究。

Abstract: We propose multiple new convex losses for SVM and Neural Networks, applied to binary classification tasks. While there are practical limitations in exploiting them with the dual SVM models, we are able to use them with SVM primal formulation and Neural Networks. In detail, the primal SVM problem with the modified losses has been solved with the Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm. We prove that the proposed losses are a generalization of the standard loss, and we experiment them with several small data-sets. This preliminary study shows that using pattern correlations
  inside the loss function could in theory enhance the generalization performances on some data-sets. To evaluate the performance of each loss, we adopt a Nested Cross-Validation procedure. Results show that generalization measures are the same with or without the new losses.

</details>


### [160] [Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303)
*Francesco Quinzan,Noor Munir,Yishun Lu,Stephen Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeSIFT，一种不依赖威胁模型的代码生成提示注入检测方法，通过影响函数测量生成代码在参数空间的影响，并利用统计检验判断候选提示集是否偏离良性参考分布，在多个代码LLM上达到最高0.98的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型用于代码生成时容易受到恶意提示攻击产生不安全代码。现有防御方法通常依赖预定义的威胁模型或已知漏洞模式，对新型攻击的泛化能力有限，需要一种不依赖先验知识的检测方法。

Method: CodeSIFT采用威胁模型无关的检测框架：首先利用影响函数（influence functions）测量生成代码在参数空间的影响，然后通过统计检验判断候选提示集是否偏离良性参考分布，从而实现异常行为检测。

Result: 在三个开源代码LLM（3B到7B参数）上评估，在中等至高注入率下AUROC最高达0.98，具有良好校准的假阳性率，并显著优于静态分析基线。同时引入了覆盖多种漏洞的基准数据集。

Conclusion: 基于影响函数的检测为识别恶意代码生成提示提供了有前景的方向，无需预先了解底层攻击类别即可有效检测。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet they remain vulnerable to prompts that elicit insecure implementations. Existing defenses typically rely on predefined threat models or known vulnerability patterns, limiting their effectiveness against novel attacks. We propose CodeSIFT, a threat-model-agnostic detection method that leverages influence functions to identify batches of prompts that induce anomalous model behavior. Rather than detecting specific vulnerabilities, CodeSIFT measures the parameter-space influence of generated code and uses a statistical test to determine whether a candidate prompt set deviates from a benign reference distribution. To evaluate our approach, we introduce two benchmark datasets covering a variety of vulnerabilities. We evaluate CodeSIFT on three open-weight code LLMs ranging from 3B to 7B parameters, achieving AUROC scores of up to 0.98 at moderate-to-high injection rates, while maintaining well-calibrated false positive rates and substantially outperforming static analysis baselines. These results suggest that influence-function-based detection is a promising direction for identifying malicious code-generation prompts without requiring prior knowledge of the underlying attack class.

</details>


### [161] [Quantum Multi-Armed Bandits and Linear Bandits: Lower Bounds and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14319)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量子多臂老虎机（QMAB）和量子线性老虎机（QLB）问题，首次证明了QMAB的极小极大下界Ω(K log(T/K))和有限动作集QLB的Ω(d log(T/d))，解决了Wan等人[2023]提出的关于T无关遗憾是否可实现的问题。同时提出了基于设计消除的有限动作集QLB算法，将维度依赖从d²改善到d（匹配下界，仅差多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作在量子奖励神谕或逆神谕模型下给出了QMAB的O(K log T)遗憾和QLB的O(d² polylog T)遗憾，但K log T规模是否不可避免、d²依赖能否改进等问题悬而未决。Wan等人[2023]提出的"遗憾能否独立于T"这一根本问题需要下界来回答。

Method: 方法论核心包括：(1) 高置信度单臂量子测试下界，通过多项式方法和适用于三角多项式的Remez型不等式证明；(2) 老虎机到测试的归约（bandit-to-testing reduction）用于提升到QMAB下界；(3) 线性嵌入得到有限动作集QLB下界；(4) 对算法侧，提出基于设计消除的算法，耦合低偏差低方差量子均值估计器与小支撑G-最优设计，并通过与设计权重匹配的查询分配实现。

Result: 建立了QMAB的Ω(K log(T/K))极小极大下界和有限动作集QLB的Ω(d log(T/d))下界。在算法侧，当动作集规模为poly(d)时，所提出的基于设计消除的算法遗憾为O(d)（线性于d），优于先前的d²依赖并匹配下界（至多差多对数因子）。结合Quantum Monte Carlo估计时可将维度依赖从d²降至d^(3/2)，低方差估计器进一步通过方差而非最坏绝对误差聚合重构误差，消除了剩余的√d因子。

Conclusion: 本文通过首次建立的极小极大下界，确认了QMAB中K log T的遗憾规模是不可避免的，并回答了Wan等人[2023]关于T无关遗憾是否可实现的开放问题。同时设计了维度最优的有限动作集QLB算法，在理论上达到了维度d的最优依赖（匹配下界），显著推进了量子老虎机问题的理解。

Abstract: We study quantum multi-armed bandits (QMAB) and quantum linear bandits (QLB) in the model of Wan et al. [2023], where the learner queries each arm or action through a quantum reward oracle or its inverse. Prior work gives algorithms over horizon $T$ with regret $O(K\log T)$ for QMAB with $K$ arms and $O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$ for $d$-dimensional QLB. This leaves open whether the $K\log T$ scale is unavoidable and whether the $d^2$ dependence can be improved. We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $Ω(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $Ω(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. A bandit-to-testing reduction then lifts it to the QMAB lower bound, while a linear embedding gives the finite-action QLB lower bound. Complementing the lower bounds, we give a design-based elimination algorithm for finite-action QLB. When the action set has size $\operatorname{poly}(d)$, its regret is linear in $d$, improving the prior $d^2$ dependence and matching our lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The algorithm couples a low-bias low-variance quantum mean estimator with a small-support $G$-optimal design through a query allocation matched to the design weights. The design-based elimination reduces the dimension dependence from $d^2$ to $d^{3/2}$ when using Quantum Monte Carlo estimates. The low-variance estimator then makes reconstruction error aggregate through variance rather than worst-case absolute error, removing the remaining $\sqrt d$ factor.

</details>


### [162] [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349)
*Michael Fore,Akshay Jain,Justin Downes,Rohan Pradhan,Duncan Botti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的轨迹预测方法，通过构建历史状态-下一位置转移表并使用乘积核检索邻居，在无GPU、零学习参数的情况下达到与57M参数Transformer相当的精度，且在数据稀缺场景下显著优于Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的轨迹预测方法（如TrAISformer）需要大量训练数据和高性能GPU资源，在数据稀缺的新地理区域部署时性能严重退化，且训练成本高。作者希望提出一种无需训练的替代方法，以极低成本实现可部署的轨迹预测。

Method: 构建历史状态到下一位置的转移表，使用乘积核（空间邻近度、方位、速度、时间上下文的乘积）检索邻居。提供两种推理模式：多样性惩罚采样（生成覆盖多条合理路径的轨迹）和束搜索（寻找最高似然路径）。

Result: 在丹麦海事AIS基准上，全数据下达到与TrAISformer相当的精度；在数据稀缺场景（训练数据降至10%）下性能稳定，而TrAISformer出现灾难性退化。

Conclusion: 该方法支持新地理区域仅需十分之一的历史数据即可部署，且完全无需GPU训练，为轨迹预测提供了一种高效、可扩展、低成本的替代方案。

Abstract: We present a training-free method for multi-modal trajectory prediction that achieves comparable accuracy to a 57M-parameter transformer while requiring no GPU and zero learned parameters. The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical data, and with no GPU training.

</details>


### [163] [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367)
*Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了业务流程中延迟案例检测的固有难度。通过对14个事件日志的研究，发现剩余时间分布通常呈强右偏，现有预测模型虽能很好地捕捉分布众数，但在高延迟案例上表现不佳。研究揭示预测不确定性与延迟幅度之间存在显著异方差性，并提出利用这一相关性来改善延迟案例的识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管剩余时间预测通过深度学习取得了显著进展，但关于延迟检测本身的固有难度知之甚少。由于通常使用聚合指标评估性能，先前工作难以揭示模型在目标分布上的表现差异，尤其是对运营最关键的（大延迟）案例。

Method: 在14个事件日志上系统分析剩余时间分布特性，考察模型在不同延迟区间上的表现；量化预测不确定性与延迟幅度的关系（异方差性）；评估缓解类别不平衡的方法；探索利用不确定性-延迟相关性来改进延迟案例识别。

Result: 剩余时间通常强右偏，仅少数案例呈现大延迟；现有模型能较好捕捉分布众数但在高延迟案例上表现差；发现显著的异方差性，预测不确定性随延迟幅度增大而增加；缓解不平衡的方法收益有限；利用不确定性-延迟相关性可显著改进延迟案例识别。

Conclusion: 延迟检测困难的核心问题可能并非类别不平衡，而是与延迟案例相关的不确定性更高。不确定性感知建模是未来预测性流程监控研究的有前景方向。

Abstract: The early detection of delayed cases in business processes is a critical capability for organizations. Predictive process monitoring (PPM) supports this task by using historical event logs to predict the remaining time of ongoing cases, enabling timely interventions to avoid missed deadlines and service level violations. Although remaining time prediction has advanced considerably through sophisticated deep learning architectures, little is known about the intrinsic difficulty of delay detection itself. Since performance is typically assessed using aggregate metrics, prior work provides limited insight into how models perform across the target distribution, especially on the operationally most critical cases with large delays. In this paper, we address this gap by analyzing the difficulty of delay detection. Across 14 event logs, we show that remaining times are typically strongly right-skewed, with only a small fraction of cases exhibiting large delays. Existing models capture the mode of this distribution well but perform poorly on high-delay cases. We further uncover pronounced heteroscedasticity, showing that predictive uncertainty increases with delay magnitude. Based on these findings, we evaluate approaches to mitigate the imbalance problem, but find only limited benefits, suggesting that the key underlying problem may not be imbalance but higher uncertainty associated with delayed cases. We show that this correlation can be exploited to substantially improve the identification of delayed cases. Overall, our work provides new insights into the sources of difficulty in delay detection and identifies uncertainty-aware modeling as a promising direction for future PPM research.

</details>


### [164] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"imposter"这一判别式自监督预训练任务，通过替换实体特征子集并训练编码器识别被交换的特征，迫使模型学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。作者在ERA5-Land再分析数据（21个环境变量）上评估该方法，并在七个下游任务（气候分类、碳通量估算、径流预测）中对比多种SSL目标，首次在统一架构和预训练预算下系统比较了陆面建模的SSL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据中实体的特征通常受物理定律共同约束，但现有自监督学习目标大多忽略了这种物理一致性。作者希望利用物理一致性作为新的自监督信号来源，帮助科学基础模型学到更真实的跨特征依赖关系。

Method: 提出imposter任务：将某个实体的部分特征替换为另一实体的真实观测值，训练编码器识别哪些特征被交换。由于每个替换值本身都是真实合理的，模型不能仅凭单特征合理性检测异常，必须学习跨特征的物理依赖才能完成该任务。

Result: 在ERA5-Land数据上对21个环境变量、7个下游任务的评估显示：最有效的预训练任务取决于下游任务家族，没有单一目标在所有任务上全面占优；imposter与现有SSL目标结合时能提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学基础模型有价值的新自监督信号来源。研究还提供了陆面建模领域首个在统一架构和预算下的SSL目标系统对比。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [165] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机权重平均（SWA）作为一种替代深度集成的方法来将对称性融入神经网络。作者用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练末期的随机训练轨迹，证明在无限宽度极限下，SWA在增强数据上能提供超出SWA本身性能提升的等变性增益，并通过大量实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强是利用对称性的有效方式，但无限深度集成虽能实现完美对称性却成本高昂（需要重复训练）。需要一种不需要多次训练就能获得对称性收益的替代方法。

Method: 将训练末期的随机权重轨迹近似为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程来分析SWA，并在无限宽度极限下推导SWA对增强数据的等变性提升。

Result: 在无限宽度极限下，SWA在增强数据上提供的等变性提升超过了仅由SWA性能提升所能预期的水平；实验在计算机视觉和图分类任务上验证了该结论，涵盖离散和连续对称性。

Conclusion: SWA是一种无需重复训练即可获得数据增强对称性收益的可行且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [166] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeaMoE，一种解码高效的混合专家（MoE）架构。该架构将专家按专业领域分组为"部门"，同一部门的专家共享大部分参数，并保留少量私有参数以体现独特性。同时设计了定制的两阶段路由策略，避免冗余权重加载，显著提升了LLM解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型被广泛用于实时交互应用（如编码助手、实时音视频交互系统），需要极低的响应延迟，通常采用小批量解码。在此场景下，MoE推理受限于内存带宽，专家权重加载成为严重瓶颈。然而现有解决方案（如训练后权重压缩、预训练中细粒度专家设计）要么损害模型精度，要么引入额外计算和通信开销。

Method: 提出DeaMoE架构：1) 将专家按专业领域分组为"部门"，同一部门的专家共享大部分参数（因来自相同专业领域），每个专家仅保留少量私有参数体现独特性；2) 设计定制的两阶段路由策略，避免冗余的权重加载，从而提升LLM解码效率。

Result: 与vanilla MoE相比，DeaMoE将每步加载权重减少最多50.9%，在A40上对预训练7B模型实现最高1.33倍端到端TPOT加速；在微基准测试中，对DeepSeek-V3在A40和H100上分别达到最高2.00倍和1.97倍峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过参数共享的部门化专家设计和两阶段路由策略，有效解决了小批量解码场景下MoE推理的内存带宽瓶颈问题，在不降低模型质量的前提下显著提升了解码效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [167] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CytoBERT，一个公开可用的单细胞流式细胞术数据基础模型。该模型通过自监督方式在15个人类数据集（超过5000万个细胞）上预训练，通过标记标准化处理异构标记面板，实现跨异质细胞术数据集的迁移学习，并在样本级分类任务中验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞术数据在免疫学研究和临床中广泛应用，但存在高度异质性和非标准化问题（源于实验方案和特征选择差异），导致机器学习方法难以跨研究应用和迁移。虽然基础模型能缓解这些问题，但该领域相关方法仍然稀缺。

Method: CytoBERT是一个开放源码、开放权重的基础模型，用于可变标记面板的单细胞细胞术数据。通过标记标准化处理，在包含15个人类数据集（异构标记面板，超过5000万个细胞）的大规模细胞术语料库上进行自监督预训练，学习细胞内可迁移的标记间关系。预训练后可针对样本级分类任务进行微调。

Result: 在样本级分类任务上微调CytoBERT表明，跨异构细胞术数据集的迁移学习是可行的，为可扩展、可泛化的细胞术分析提供了起点。

Conclusion: CytoBERT证明了自监督基础模型能够学习跨异构细胞术数据集的标记间关系，实现有效的迁移学习，为该领域的可扩展通用分析奠定基础。代码已在GitHub上公开。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [168] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Power Sampling在推理时增强中的一个悖论：虽然它能将更多概率质量推向正确轨迹，却可能降低其本应增强的下游推理性能（如自一致性准确率最多下降18.5个百分点）。作者将问题归因于剂量不匹配和覆盖不匹配，并提出了一种保留支持集的、受变形控制的Power目标来修复该问题，在相同预算下优于标准多样本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling作为一种无需验证器的推理时增强方法，有望成为多种下游采样方法的通用前端。然而作者发现了令人惊讶的悖论——它可能损害其想要增强的下游推理，这挑战了该方法作为通用前端的有效性，需要诊断并修复这一根本性问题。

Method: 1. 通过自一致性作为代表性案例，量化Power Sampling导致的准确率下降（最高18.5个百分点）。2. 将悖论归因于两种不匹配：剂量不匹配（固定指数在不同问题上导致差异巨大的分布变化）和覆盖不匹配（全局锐化将质量集中在少数主导路径上，虽然pass@k高但丧失了广泛推理路径的支持）。3. 提出修复方案：用变形控制、保留支持集的Power目标替代均匀轨迹幂运算，跨问题校准锐化程度并限制中等概率路径的过度抑制。

Result: 在相同预算的加权自一致性实现中，修复后的采样器逆转了全局Power造成的损失，并在多个推理基准上优于标准多样本推理。

Conclusion: Power Sampling的全局锐化策略存在根本性的覆盖损失问题，通过受控的、保留支持集的锐化方式可以在保持推理路径多样性的同时实现有效的概率质量重分配，从而使Power类方法真正成为可靠的下游推理前端。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [169] [Designing Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models: A Unified Path-Space View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14430)
*Yixian Xu,Yuanrui Zhang,Shengjie Luo,Liwei Wang,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了扩散模型RL后训练的两类方法（反向轨迹法与正向匹配法），证明它们源于同一路径空间原则，并提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器与有界权重族，在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的RL后训练算法目前碎片化，不同方法间缺乏统一的理论框架，难以系统性比较和改进。

Method: 从正则化扩散RL目标出发，利用采样SDE间的重要性采样获得轨迹空间上的显式策略梯度估计器；推导出等价的方差缩减值梯度形式（恢复AWM和DiffusionNFT的正向匹配结构）；提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器与尺度有界权重族。

Result: 实验验证了方差缩减解释，所提方法在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image上优于现有扩散RL基线。

Conclusion: 两类方法的经验差距本质上是方差缩减效应而非RL原则差异，统一的设计空间可指导更优的扩散RL算法设计。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training provides a direct way to align diffusion models with human preferences and task-specific rewards. However, current RL algorithms for diffusion models remain fragmented: reverse-trajectory methods rely on discretized likelihood ratios, whereas forward-matching methods train on reward-labeled noising versions of the rollout samples. This paper shows that these seemingly different losses arise from a single path-space principle. Starting from the regularized diffusion-RL objective, we use importance sampling between sampling SDEs to obtain an explicit policy-gradient estimator on trajectory space. The estimator contains the stochastic Itô integral underlying Flow-GRPO-type updates; we derive an equivalent variance-reduced value-gradient form that recovers the forward-matching structure of AWM and DiffusionNFT. This identifies the empirical gap between these method families as a variance-reduction effect rather than a difference in RL principle. The derivation yields a unified design space organized by value-gradient estimation, weight functions, and sampling choices. Within this space, we propose a multi-sample KDE value-gradient estimator that reuses rollout groups, together with scale-bounded weight families that retain stable existing recipes while excluding singular ones. Experiments on SD3.5-M and Qwen-Image models validate the variance-reduction explanation and show that the resulting recipe improves over prior diffusion-RL baselines.

</details>


### [170] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用的双层优化框架用于神经架构搜索（NAS），通过三种可扩展的连续松弛公式（神经元门控NAS-NG、混合激活NAS-MA、两者结合NAS-NGMA）替代离散的神经元和激活层决策，在MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等架构上实现可微优化，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上取得竞争性或更优的性能，同时显著减少参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NAS被形式化为双层优化问题，但由于离散的架构决策、指数级增长的搜索空间以及训练候选架构的高成本，导致计算开销巨大。需要一种可扩展且通用的方法来替代离散NAS，实现高效的架构优化。

Method: 提出三种连续松弛的NAS方法：1) NAS-NG（神经元门控）：对神经元进行连续门控松弛；2) NAS-MA（混合激活）：对激活函数进行连续混合松弛；3) NAS-NGMA：结合神经元门控和混合激活。三种方法均将离散的架构决策转化为可微的连续优化问题，在统一的双层优化框架下进行求解。

Result: 在MNIST上，NAS-NGMA以7.69M MLP参数达到98.68%测试精度，NAS-NG仅以0.26M CNN参数达到99.63%精度；在CIFAR-10上，所提方法一致优于vanilla DARTS。此外，NAS-NG能优化过参数化和文献最优的架构，在降低参数的同时提升精度。

Conclusion: 松弛双层优化可作为离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，为神经元和激活层面的高效架构优化提供了通用框架，在多种架构类型上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [171] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一种基于线性规划的微分神经架构搜索框架。LP-NAS利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian构建线性规划，计算既能提升泛化性能又不损害模型参数最优性的架构更新方向。作者提出了S-LP-NAS和R-LP-NAS两个高效变体，并将其应用于DARTS搜索空间得到S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS。实验表明，LP-DARTS在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上优于标准DARTS，且优于P-DARTS、PC-DARTS和STO-DARTS等多个变体，并在ImageNet上验证了架构的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分NAS将搜索空间松弛为连续域，因此可以应用连续优化的原理。然而现有微分NAS方法在架构优化过程中缺乏系统的数学规划框架，导致收敛慢且早期搜索性能不佳。作者希望通过构建线性规划来更高效地求解架构更新方向，从而加速并改善架构搜索过程。

Method: LP-NAS将NAS问题转化为线性规划问题：利用验证损失的梯度信息以及训练损失的Hessian矩阵来构造LP约束和目标，求解出的架构更新方向既能改善泛化性能，又能保持模型参数的最优性。作者进一步提出两种计算高效的变体：S-LP-NAS（简化版本）和R-LP-NAS（正则化版本），并将其嵌入DARTS搜索空间形成S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，LP-DARTS在架构搜索和评估阶段均优于标准DARTS，且早期搜索迭代中收敛更快、验证性能显著更高。在CIFAR-10上与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS和STO-DARTS对比中展现有效性。此外，在ImageNet上的实验验证了发现架构的迁移性。

Conclusion: LP-NAS证明了数学规划（特别是线性规划）可以有效地应用于微分NAS，为架构搜索提供了一种理论上更严谨、实践中更高效的优化框架。该方法不仅加速了架构搜索过程，还提升了最终架构的质量和可迁移性，为未来将更多连续优化技术引入NAS领域提供了新思路。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [172] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出sMuon方法，通过线性化和最小二乘近似解决Muon优化器与LoRA低秩参数化无法直接结合的问题，在SFT微调和ReLoRA预训练实验中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在神经网络预训练中表现出明显优势，但在参数高效微调(PEFT)中应用较少。主要原因是LoRA的低秩参数化在数学上无法直接对权重更新进行正交化，因此需要一种方法将Muon的优势扩展到低秩微调场景。

Method: 通过线性化将一个松弛的Muon目标近似求解到低秩设置中，然后利用最小二乘法进行求解。实现方法仅使用矩阵乘法(matmul)运算，而非更复杂的线性代数分解例程。

Result: sMuon在SFT微调和ReLoRA预训练实验中表现良好，总体上发现Muon用于低秩微调能带来中等程度的性能提升，但结果依赖于具体模型和评测任务。

Conclusion: sMuon成功将Muon优化器的优势扩展到LoRA等低秩微调场景，提供了一种高效且有效的替代方案，且仅需简单的矩阵乘法运算即可实现。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [173] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段跨表数据生成（CTDG）框架，将多个异质表格转化为标准化统计表，再利用扩散Transformer生成合成统计表，最后通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建合成原始表格，实现从多个异质表学习统一生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成表格数据生成方法仅限于单输入表场景，难以有效处理具有不同特征集的多个异质表格，缺乏跨表数据生成能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段将每个异质原始表转换为具有相同列集的标准化统计表，捕获边际分布和两两相关性；第二阶段训练扩散Transformer模型学习统计表间的结构模式并生成合成统计表，再通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建合成原始表。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在学习到的统计表示上具有较高保真度，并在生成合成数据时取得了良好的保真度-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架能够从多个异质表学习统一生成模型，并支持生成无限数量的真实异构合成表，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [174] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rollplex是一个针对视觉语言模型（VLM）强化学习（RL）后训练的高效运行时系统。它通过将参考和训练阶段分解，并将前缀计算移入rollout解码窗口，实现了阶段间流水线重叠，同时保持同步on-policy语义。在32块H800 GPU上，相比串行部署获得1.23×–1.30×加速，相比分离部署获得1.57×–2.24×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy RL运行时按严格串行阶段执行rollout、参考评分和actor训练，这对文本RL有效，但对VLM非常浪费。VLM处理密集视频输入和提示前缀占用了每个阶段的大部分时间，而前缀处理独立于生成响应，可以在rollout解码的同时运行，以避免GPU计算能力利用不足的问题。

Method: Rollplex提出两种核心机制：(1) 相位感知内存管理（Phase-aware memory management），根据生产者-消费者生命周期控制HBM驻留；(2) 并行感知权重共享（Parallelism-aware weight sharing），对不同张量并行度下布局兼容的张量使用同一物理存储，仅对不兼容张量重建，避免完整的第二份actor副本。这解决了将rollout和训练共置时的大内存需求（Qwen2.5-VL-32B需要约165 GiB/GPU）以及不同TP度和权重布局偏好的问题。

Result: 在32块H800 GPU上，Rollplex在保持同步RL更新的同时，相比串行同地部署实现1.23×–1.30×加速，相比相同GPU预算下的分离式部署实现1.57×–2.24×加速。

Conclusion: Rollplex证明，通过将前缀计算与rollout解码重叠，并采用相位感知内存管理和并行感知权重共享，可以在不破坏同步on-policy语义的前提下，显著提升VLM强化学习后训练的效率。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [175] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecipeNet是一种分层Transformer架构，专门用于配方数据（如材料合成、药物配方、工业制造）的表示学习。它通过堆叠的Transformer编码器分别捕获步骤内字段级交互和步骤间顺序依赖，在多个配方数据集和任务上优于现有表格学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格学习方法在处理配方数据时，通常将有序步骤序列中的异构结构化字段扁平化为固定模式表示，无法有效捕获层级字段交互和过程依赖关系，因此需要一种专门针对配方数据结构的建模方法。

Method: RecipeNet采用分层Transformer架构：底层Transformer编码器负责捕获每个步骤内部的字段级交互，顶层Transformer编码器则建模步骤之间的顺序依赖关系，通过堆叠的方式实现层级建模。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet一致性地优于现有的表格模型，验证了分层和顺序建模对于配方表示学习的价值。

Conclusion: 分层与顺序建模是配方数据表示学习的关键，RecipeNet的有效性表明针对结构化过程数据设计专门架构比通用表格方法更具优势。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Fine-Tuning Qwen3-27B for C-to-Rust Code Translation: A Three-Stage Curriculum of Pretraining, Debugging-Aware SFT, and Task-Specific SFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13681)
*Pu Zhao,Changdi Yang,Yixiao Chen,Yi Gao,Yifan Cao,Haochen Zeng,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段微调课程，将Qwen3-27B大语言模型逐步专门化为C到Rust代码翻译任务。该课程包括：持续预训练（强化Rust语法先验）、在Verus_Training_Data上的监督微调（培养调试和自修复能力）、以及在LeetCode配对C/Rust解决方案上的任务特定微调（教学直接语义翻译）。模型在SACTOR框架下评估，显示出优于基线模型的翻译成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将C代码翻译为安全、惯用的Rust是长期追求的软件工程目标，可以消除内存安全漏洞类别。然而，通用LLM在开箱即用时表现不佳，因为预训练很少强调惯用Rust生成、跨语言语义等价性以及根据编译器/运行时反馈进行推理和修复的能力。

Method: 采用三阶段微调课程：(1) 在Rust中心语料上持续预训练，增强模型对惯用Rust语法和标准库使用的先验；(2) 在microsoft/Verus_Training_Data数据集上进行监督微调，灌输对Rust代码的调试和自修复行为；(3) 在源自LeetCode问题的配对C/Rust解决方案上进行任务特定监督微调，教学直接语义翻译。评估使用SACTOR框架，该框架执行结构感知的两阶段（非惯用到惯用）翻译，并采用基于FFI的端到端测试。

Result: 使用SACTOR框架评估模型，报告成功率、惯用性（Clippy lint计数、不安全代码占比）和失败模式分析，并与基线Qwen3-27B及同一框架下的其他LLM进行比较。

Conclusion: 三阶段微调课程能有效将通用LLM专门化为C到Rust翻译任务，通过分步专业化逐步提升模型在惯用性、语义等价性和自我修复方面的能力。

Abstract: Translating C code into safe, idiomatic Rust is a longstanding software-engineering goal because it can eliminate entire classes of memory-safety vulnerabilities while preserving the functional behavior of legacy systems. Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for this task but typically underperform when applied off-the-shelf, since general-purpose pretraining rarely emphasizes idiomatic Rust generation, cross-language semantic equivalence, or the ability to reason about and repair compiler/runtime feedback. In this report we describe a three-stage fine-tuning curriculum applied to Qwen3-27B that is designed to progressively specialize the model for the C-to-Rust (C2Rust) translation task: (1) continued pretraining on Rust-centric corpora to strengthen the model's prior over idiomatic Rust syntax and standard-library usage; (2) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on the microsoft/Verus_Training_Data dataset to instill debugging and self-repair behavior over Rust code; and (3) task-specific SFT on paired C/Rust solutions derived from LeetCode problems to teach direct semantic translation. We evaluate the resulting model using the agentic, static-analysis-guided verification framework of SACTOR, which performs structure-aware, two-phase (unidiomatic to idiomatic) translation with foreign-function-interface (FFI)-based end-to-end (E2E) testing. We report success rate, idiomaticity (Clippy lint counts, unsafe-code fraction), and failure-mode analyses, and compare our fine-tuned model against baseline Qwen3-27B and other LLMs evaluated under the same framework.

</details>


### [177] [Building AI-Intensive Software with AI: Early Results and a Cautionary Tale on Measuring Development Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13730)
*Victor Barros de Miranda Neves,Kiev Santos da Gama,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项关于AI密集型软件开发的真实成本案例研究：六人学生团队在一个学期内利用AI辅助构建了完整的对话式入职助手。研究使用三层成本模型（实际AI支出、自报人力投入、人力反事实），最初报告的19.4倍成本比率在复查后发现两处独立错误（订阅制下按token费用推断、错误的地域劳动力费率），修正后约为9.9倍。作者将该修正本身作为一项可推广的发现——这类错误易于发生、在最终数字中不可见，且可能普遍存在于类似报告中，并提出了更稳健的成本核算方法学方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于AI密集型软件开发真实成本的实证报告非常稀缺，且现有少数报告容易在最终数字中隐藏计算错误。作者希望通过细致的案例研究提供准确的成本数据，并揭示成本核算中的常见陷阱。

Method: 采用进行中的案例研究方法：六人学生团队用AI辅助构建完整对话式入职助手（RAG代码聊天、引导式导览、依赖图、技术债务分析）。开发过程使用三层成本模型（实际AI支出、自报人力工时、人力反事实成本）进行量化，并对初步结果进行复核修正。

Result: 最初报告19.4倍的成本比率，复核后发现两处错误：在固定费率订阅下推断逐token成本，以及采用错误地域劳动力费率给反事实定价，两处错误合计将比率夸大约2倍。修正后的成本比率约为9.9倍。

Conclusion: 该修正本身是一项可推广的早期发现：这两种错误都容易犯、在最终数字中不可见、且可能在类似报告中普遍存在。作者计划推进更稳健、可复现的AI密集型开发成本核算方法学。

Abstract: Empirical reports on the true cost of AI-intensive software development remain scarce, and the few that exist are easy to get wrong in ways that never surface in the final number. We report early results from an ongoing case study: a six-person student team built a full conversational onboarding assistant -- RAG-based code chat, guided tours, dependency graphs, technical-debt analysis -- over one academic term using pervasive AI assistance. We instrumented development with a three-layer cost model (real AI spend, self-reported human effort, human counterfactual) and initially reported a 19.4x cost ratio. A follow-up pass revealed two independent errors -- inferring per-token cost under a flat-rate subscription, and pricing the counterfactual with the wrong regional labor rates -- that together had inflated the ratio by roughly 2x; the corrected figure is ~9.9x. We present this correction as an early, generalizable finding in its own right: both errors are easy to make, invisible in the final number, and plausibly common in similar reports. We outline next steps toward a more robust, replicable costing methodology for AI-intensive development.

</details>


### [178] [Does ISO-Grounded NFR Specification Improve LLM Code Generation? A Comparison of Rich and Structured Interventions against a Natural-Language Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13742)
*Joào Pedro Monteiro Pereira,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究在LLM代码生成中，将非功能需求（NFR）规范基于ISO/IEC 25010质量模型进行丰富（无论是自然语言散文还是结构化JSON）是否能改善生成代码的质量。研究发现：ISO丰富的NFR能改善静态质量代理指标并降低对提示词措辞的敏感性，但不能可靠提高功能正确性；当ISO内容恒定时，NL-rich与Structured格式差异可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码生成中，NFR通常以简短的单行短语形式被指定（如"性能好"、"错误处理完善"），这种简略表达可能导致模型无法充分理解和执行非功能需求。作者希望探究：如果将NFR规格基于ISO/IEC 25010标准进行grounding（丰富化），无论是用丰富的自然语言还是结构化JSON，是否比简单的单行描述更能改善生成的代码质量。

Method: 在HumanEval/HumanEval-ET基准上，评估四种NFR（性能、错误处理、代码异味、可读性），对比三种条件：RobuNFR风格单行基线（NL-simple）、ISO丰富的自然语言散文（NL-rich）、结构化JSON（Structured）。每种条件使用10种提示词变体，固定模型快照，采用配对非参数统计分析。

Result: 主要发现：ISO丰富的NFR改善了静态质量代理指标（不可读性密度在四种NFR中均下降，如性能从0.88降至0.69），并降低了对提示词措辞的敏感性，但不能可靠提高功能正确性；错误处理的扩展测试通过率反而下降，表明防御性编码模式与精确输出基准之间存在张力。次要发现：当ISO内容恒定时，NL-rich和Structured在正确性上差异极小（|delta|<=0.023），表明语义内容比JSON与散文格式更重要。

Conclusion: 实践者应投资于基于标准grounded的NFR内容本身，而非序列化形式（JSON vs 散文）。提供了完全可追踪的复现包。

Abstract: In LLM-based code generation, Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) are often specified as terse one-line phrases. We ask whether grounding those specifications in ISO/IEC 25010 Quality Model, either as rich natural-language prose (NL-rich) or as structured JSON (Structured), improves code generated on HumanEval/HumanEval-ET compared to a RobuNFR-style one-line baseline (NL-simple). We evaluate four NFRs (performance, error handling, code smell, readability) with ten prompt variations per condition under a fixed model snapshot and paired non-parametric analysis. Primary finding: ISO-grounded enrichment improves static quality proxies (unreadability density falls across all four NFRs (e.g., Performance 0.88 -> 0.69 for NL-rich)) and reduces sensitivity to prompt wording, but does not reliably improve functional correctness; for error handling, extended-test pass rate decreases, suggesting tension between defensive coding patterns and exact-output benchmarks. Secondary finding: when ISO content is held constant, NL-rich and Structured differ negligibly in correctness (|delta| <= 0.023), indicating that semantic content matters more than JSON-vs-prose format. Practitioners should invest in standard-grounded NFR content rather than serialization form. A fully traceable replication package is provided.

</details>


### [179] [Engineering Reliable Coding Agents: Evaluating and Operating the System Around the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13867)
*Stephanie Jarmak*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于AI编码代理系统的专题综述，指出编码代理的可靠性不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于整个系统架构（如执行环境、检索、状态管理、权限、审查接口等）。通过多声部综述（164篇学术文献、100篇实践记录、29个基准记录、17个作者系统案例），建立了评估与操作系统级可靠性的框架，贡献了206条可靠性记录的版本化目录、证据账本、依赖链框架、可运行协议及5个可复用代理技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理通常作为模型被评估，但作为系统被部署。其可靠性取决于整个基础设施而非仅模型能力。许多表面上的模型失败实际上源于系统其他层，且某一层的改进往往无法传导至端到端结果。现有评估方法缺乏对系统级因素的考量，需要建立区分模型能力与基础设施影响的系统级方法论。

Method: 采用结构化多声部综述方法，综合164篇学术文献、100份实践记录、29份基准记录和17份作者系统案例；辅以定向更新审计、软件工程覆盖率分析和分布式系统证据综合。将评估与运维视为依赖链，对任务构建、执行环境、检索、状态管理、验证和可观测性各环节进行分析。

Result: 构建了包含206条可靠性记录的版本化目录（193条门控实践，其中56条深入开发；13条研究线索）、证据账本、跨代理生命周期的依赖与修复不对称框架、运营代理系统的测量与故障案例、可运行的评估与可靠性协议，以及带证据图的5个可复用代理技能。

Conclusion: 提供了区分模型能力与基础设施效应的系统级方法论，可用于设计可辩护的评估并构建组件故障时可安全恢复的系统。综述是结构化而非穷尽式的，证据强度因主题而异，结果依赖于工作负载与配置。

Abstract: AI coding agents are commonly evaluated as models but deployed as systems. Their reliability depends not only on model capability, but on the harness, execution state, retrieval, memory and state management, permissions, review interfaces, and resource allocation. This monograph examines those boundaries and develops a framework for evaluating and operating coding agents reliably. It synthesizes 164 scholarly works, 100 practitioner records, 29 benchmark records, and 17 author-system case records through a structured multivocal review, targeted update audits, software-engineering coverage analysis, and distributed-systems evidence synthesis. Across this evidence, many apparent model failures originate elsewhere in the system, while improvements at one layer often fail to propagate to end-to-end outcomes. Evaluation and operation are treated as a dependency chain in which weaknesses in task construction, execution environments, retrieval, state management, verification, or observability can invalidate downstream conclusions. The monograph contributes a versioned catalog of 206 reliability records: 193 gated practices, including 56 developed in depth, plus 13 research leads; an evidence ledger; a framework for dependency and repair asymmetry across the agent lifecycle; measurements and failure cases from operated agent systems; runnable evaluation and reliability protocols; and five reusable agent skills with evidence maps. Together, these provide a system-level methodology for distinguishing model capability from infrastructure effects, designing defensible evaluations, and building systems that recover safely when components fail. The review is structured rather than exhaustive, evidence strength varies by topic, and results depend on workload and configuration. The methods record which search lanes were executed, which remain unexecuted, and limits on evidence-grading claims.

</details>


### [180] [Engineering Signals of Human-AI Collaboration in the Agentic Coding Era: A Longitudinal Analysis of 33,228 Pull Requests from vLLM and SGLang with Implications for Biomedical AI Agents and Bioinformatics Pipeline Developmen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13884)
*Jiada Li,Xuesong Ye,Olamide Olowoniyi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对两个高速度AI基础设施开源仓库（vLLM和SGLang）进行纵向描述性分析，追踪七项工程指标（PR吞吐量、周期时间、贡献者多样性、PR评论密度、合并率、新作者参与度和PR规模）在四个AI辅助开发时代的变化。结果显示PR吞吐量增长21倍和17.9倍，且增长主要由人类驱动（机器人PR占比<0.2%），同时贡献者参与度扩大、评论密度增加，但PR规模保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手和自主代理开发系统的快速普及与开源软件工程步伐和结构的变化相吻合，但在团队层面缺乏经验性的纵向证据来量化这些变化。现有研究多为横断面或短期观察，无法揭示AI辅助开发对工程团队指标的长周期影响。

Method: 对vLLM（2023年2月-2026年6月，18,290个PR）和SGLang（2024年1月-2026年6月，14,938个PR）中的所有合并PR计算七项工程指标，按AI辅助软件开发重大变化划分四个时代，分别考察人类和机器人撰写的活动。

Result: 两个项目均显示开发速度和AI开发者协作显著提升。vLLM PR吞吐量增长21倍，SGLang增长17.9倍，其中机器人PR占比不足0.2%，增长几乎完全由人类驱动。最新时代中位周期时间为vLLM 1.04天、SGLang 0.62天，P90周期时间分别达16.8和14.3天。月度独立作者持续增长。PR评论密度分别增长4.2倍和3.8倍，机器人评论贡献约15-20%的增幅。PR规模在各时代保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发与高速度开源软件开发中的更高吞吐量、更广泛的贡献者参与以及增强的AI开发者协作信号相关联。研究提供了团队层面AI对工程实践影响的实证证据，但增长的人类驱动本质表明AI是赋能而非替代人类开发者。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI coding assistants and autonomous agentic development systems has coincided with major changes in the pace and structure of open-source software engineering. Yet empirical longitudinal evidence of these changes at the team level remains limited. We present a descriptive longitudinal analysis of seven engineering metrics: pull request (PR) throughput, cycle time, contributor diversity, PR comment density, merge rate, new-author participation, and PR size. Metrics were computed from all merged PRs in two high-velocity AI infrastructure repositories, vLLM (February 2023-June 2026; 18,290 PRs) and SGLang (January 2024-June 2026; 14,938 PRs). We segment development into four eras aligned with major changes in AI-assisted software development and examine human- and bot-authored activities. Both projects show substantial increases in development velocity and AI-developer collaboration signals. PR throughput increased 21x in vLLM and 17.9x in SGLang, while bot-authored PRs accounted for less than 0.2% of this growth, indicating that the increase was overwhelmingly human-driven. In the latest era, median cycle time was 1.04 days for vLLM and 0.62 days for SGLang, while P90 cycle times reached 16.8 and 14.3 days, respectively. Monthly unique authors increased steadily in both projects, suggesting broader contributor participation. PR comment density increased 4.2x in vLLM and 3.8x in SGLang, with bot comments contributing an estimated 15-20% of the increase. In contrast, PR size remained relatively stable across eras. Overall, AI-assisted development is associated with higher throughput, broader contributor participation, and increased AI-developer collaboration signals in high-velocity open-source software development.

</details>


### [181] [Rethinking Automated Program Repair: The Impact of Bug Complexity, Fault Localization, and LLM Cost-efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14065)
*Junchi Liu,Ali Bigdeli,Roya Daneshi,Atu Ambala,Sudipto Ghosh,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动程序修复（APR）技术进行了全面的实证分析，评估了两种APR技术（ChatRepair和CodeCorrector）在三种LLM（DeepSeek、GPT、Llama）上的表现，重点关注bug复杂度、故障定位、推理设置和修复成本效率等因素对修复性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based APR研究主要关注整体修复有效性，而对bug复杂度、故障定位精度、推理设置以及修复成本效率等因素的影响缺乏系统探索，需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用多维实证框架和统计分析，评估两种APR技术（ChatRepair和CodeCorrector）配合三种LLM（DeepSeek、GPT、Llama）的性能，考察不同bug复杂度水平和故障定位策略下的修复表现。

Result: 结构复杂的bug和不精确的故障定位会使修复更具挑战性，但LLM-based APR仍能达到有竞争力的修复效果。不精确的故障定位会显著扩大APR技术之间的性能差距。此外，更高成本的LLM和更强的推理设置并不总带来更好的成本效率。GPT-5比DeepSeek-V4-pro和DeepSeek-V3.2分别多修复7个和39个复杂bug，但DeepSeek-V3.2的总修复成本效率最佳。

Conclusion: 超过50%的中等复杂度bug可以由低成本LLM-based APR技术修复；修复有效性差距随着故障定位精度的降低而增大；在修复效果和计算成本之间存在着非平凡（nontrivial）的权衡。

Abstract: Background: Software bugs remain a critical challenge in development, necessitating effective Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based APR systems have shown promise, prior studies primarily focus on overall repair effectiveness. The effects of bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and repair cost-effectiveness remain insufficiently explored.
  Aims: This study presents a comprehensive empirical analysis of LLM-based APR, focusing on how repair performance is shaped by bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and costs.
  Method: We evaluate two APR techniques (ChatRepair and CodeCorrector) using three LLMs (DeepSeek, GPT, and Llama), and examine their performance across diverse levels of bug complexity and localization strategies through a multi-dimensional empirical framework and statistical analysis.
  Results: Although structurally complex bugs and imprecise fault localization make repair more challenging, LLM-based APR techniques still achieve competitive repair effectiveness. Imprecise fault localization can substantially enlarge the performance gap between APR techniques. Furthermore, higher-cost LLMs and stronger reasoning settings do not consistently yield better cost-efficiency, revealing a nontrivial trade-off between repair effectiveness and computational cost.
  Conclusions: Over 50% of moderately complex bugs can be repaired by low-cost LLM-based APR techniques. The repair effectiveness gap between APR techniques becomes larger as fault localization becomes less precise. GPT-5 repairs 7 and 39 more complex bugs than DeepSeek-V4-pro and DeepSeek-V3.2, respectively; whereas the total repair cost of DeepSeek-V3.2 shows the best cost-efficiency performance.

</details>


### [182] [DepWareTrans: Dependency-Aware Incremental Repository Migration across Co-executable Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14128)
*Sivajeet Chand,Alexander Pretschner,Steve Haupt,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对仓库级别的代码翻译问题，提出了一种依赖感知的增量迁移框架。与传统的文件级翻译方法不同，该方法将翻译单元从单个文件提升到依赖一致的批次，通过构建依赖图分组相互依赖的文件，并采用迭代编译和测试驱动的验证。在51K行代码的工业系统（STAR）及多种可互操作语言对上的实验表明，该方法达到了100%的编译和测试成功率，而文件级方法仅为38.16%的编译成功率和9.39%的测试成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的代码翻译方法在文件级别操作，无法扩展到具有复杂跨文件依赖关系的代码库。在工业场景中（将生产仓库从Java迁移到Kotlin），文件级方法产生碎片化结果，无法实现端到端的正确性。作者通过实证研究发现，依赖不一致性是仓库级别翻译失败的主要原因，迭代反馈无法有效解决跨文件依赖问题。

Method: 提出依赖感知的增量迁移框架：(1) 构建依赖图；(2) 将相互依赖的文件分组为依赖一致的批次；(3) 执行批次级翻译；(4) 使用迭代编译和测试驱动的验证来确保正确性。该方法将翻译的基本单元从单个文件提升到批次层面。

Result: 在51K LOC的工业系统STAR上，文件级方法仅实现38.16%的编译成功率和9.39%的测试成功率，而该方法在所有评估设置下均达到100%的编译和测试成功率，且收敛所需迭代次数很少。方法还在Java-Kotlin、Java-Scala和C#-F#等多个可互操作语言对上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 依赖感知的批量翻译能够显著提高仓库级别代码翻译的可扩展性和可靠性，有效解决跨文件依赖不一致这一核心障碍。

Abstract: Repository-level code translation is critical for modernizing legacy systems, yet existing approaches based on large language models (LLMs) operate at the file level and fail to scale to codebases with complex inter-file dependencies. This limitation is evident in our industrial setting, where we aim to migrate a production repository (STAR) from Java to Kotlin, but file-level approaches produce fragmented results and fail to achieve end-to-end correctness. In this paper, we show that the primary cause of failure at the repository level is dependency inconsistency. Through an empirical study on open-source and industrial systems, we find that most errors arise from unresolved cross-file dependencies that cannot be effectively addressed by iterative feedback alone. We propose a dependency-aware incremental migration framework that elevates the unit of translation from individual files to dependency-consistent batches. Our approach constructs a dependency graph, groups interdependent files, and performs batched translation with iterative compile- and test-driven validation. We evaluate our method on a 51K line of code (LOC) industrial system and multiple repositories across interoperable language pairs (Java-Kotlin, Java-Scala, and C#-F#). On the STAR repository, file-level approaches achieve 38.16% compilation and 9.39% test success, whereas our approach achieves 100% compilation and test success across the evaluated settings, converging within a small number of iterations. These results show that dependency-aware batching improves scalability and reliability in repository-level code translation.

</details>


### [183] [LegacyWorld: Atomicity-Aware Evaluation of GUI Agents for Legacy Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14131)
*Thilo Reintjes,Sivajeet Chand,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey,Alexander Pretschner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文围绕legacy-use框架的预部署评估研究，提出用原子性（atomicity）标准来评估计算机使用代理（computer-use agents）在传统企业系统GUI工作流自动化中的表现。研究构建了28个Windows GUI工作流基准，包含初始状态、目标状态和验证器，对比专家精心设计的提示与从屏幕录制生成的提示，评估了六个托管代理，发现"有用完成"、"安全失败"和"非原子副作用"是三种不同的操作特征，主张将工作流捕获、状态验证器和原子性感知验收测试作为AI遗留工作流自动化的头等需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统和类传统企业系统常因关键工作流仅提供有限编程接口、仍需手动GUI交互而难以现代化。作者在开发legacy-use框架时发现，成功演示不足以验证代理可靠性——失败的代理运行仍可能在业务或医疗记录中留下持久的无效更改，因此需要以原子性标准评估代理行为，防止失败的运行产生非预期的持久副作用。

Method: 作者构建了一个由领域专家参与的28个Windows GUI工作流基准，每个工作流包含初始状态、目标状态和任务特定验证器。采用专家精心制作的提示与从专家金路径执行的屏幕录制生成的提示进行对比，在六个托管计算机使用代理上评估原子性（运行要么正确完成预期工作流，要么失败且不产生非预期持久副作用）。

Result: 在六个托管计算机使用代理上的评估结果表明，有用完成、安全失败和非原子副作用是三种截然不同的操作特征（distinct operational profiles），即代理可能完成任务但在失败时留下副作用，或安全失败但完成率低等不同组合。

Conclusion: 工作流捕获、状态验证器和原子性感知的验收测试应成为基于AI的遗留工作流自动化的头等需求，而非可选的附加功能，以确保代理运行在失败时不会对业务和医疗记录造成持久性破坏。

Abstract: Legacy and legacy-like enterprise systems often remain difficult to modernize because critical workflows expose limited programmable interfaces and still require manual GUI interaction. This paper reports a pre-deployment evaluation study motivated by the development of legacy-use, an industry-oriented framework for automating such workflows with multimodal LLM agents. During framework development, domain experts helped identify stateful workflows where successful demos are not sufficient: a failed agent run may still leave persistent invalid changes in business or healthcare records. We therefore evaluate computer-use agents using atomicity: a run should either complete the intended workflow correctly or fail without unintended persistent side effects. We construct a domain-expert-informed benchmark of 28 Windows GUI workflows, each specified with an initial state, goal state, and task-specific validator. We compare expert-crafted prompts with prompts generated from screen recordings of expert golden-path executions. Across six hosted computer-use agents, our results show that useful completion, safe failure, and non-atomic side effects are distinct operational profiles. We conclude that workflow capture, state validators, and atomicity-aware acceptance tests should be first-class requirements for AI-based legacy workflow automation.

</details>


### [184] [Breaking Models to Test the Judge: A Mutation Testing Approach for Semantic Evaluators of Domain Class Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14315)
*Kevin Delcourt,Meriem Ben Chaaben,Abdelhamid Rouatbi,Luciano Marchezan,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将变异测试作为评估语义判断器（如基于LLM的）的可扩展替代方案。通过向领域类图注入受控语义缺陷（如删除类），生成变异体来评估候选判断器检测缺陷的能力。定义了11种变异算子，并在3个LLM和2种提示变体共6种配置下验证，结果表明自动化变异测试与人工评估在识别更优配置方面基本一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中许多语义建模任务缺乏唯一的地面真值（ground truth），人工判断既昂贵又主观。因此需要一种可扩展、客观的方法来评估语义判断器的质量。

Method: 1) 从PlantUML类图和文本系统描述对出发；2) 应用11种变异算子（如删除类）注入受控语义缺陷，生成错误变体；3) 候选判断器通过检测注入缺陷的能力被评估；4) 与常规人工评估判断有效性进行对比验证。

Result: 在6种判断器配置（3个LLM×2种提示变体）下，自动化变异测试方法与人工评估在识别表现更优配置方面大体一致。

Conclusion: 变异测试可作为分析语义判断器的可扩展代理（scalable proxy），为缺乏唯一地面真值的语义建模评估提供了一种替代方案。

Abstract: In software engineering, many semantic modeling tasks lack a unique ground truth, as human judgments are both costly and subjective. This paper explores mutation testing as a scalable alternative for evaluating semantic judges (e.g., LLM-based) of models. We propose a mutation testing approach in which controlled semantic defects are injected into domain class diagrams. Starting from pairs of PlantUML class diagrams and textual system descriptions, we apply mutation operators (e.g., removing a class) to generate faulty variants. A candidate judge is then evaluated based on its ability to detect the injected defects. We define 11 mutation operators for the task of comparing a domain class diagram against a textual description and evaluate the proposed approach against a conventional manual assessment of judgment validity. Across six judge configurations (three LLMs and two prompt variants), the automated mutation testing approach is largely consistent with the manual assessment in identifying the better-performing configurations. The results suggest that mutation testing may serve as a scalable proxy for analyzing semantic judges.

</details>


### [185] [ATLAS: Discovering Agent Strategies through LLM-Guided Abstraction and Automata Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14352)
*Ignacio D. Lopez-Miguel,Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito,Ezio Bartocci,Bettina Könighofer,Martin Tappler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS框架，通过轨迹抽象与自动机学习，从LLM智能体的执行轨迹中恢复可解释的有限状态行为模型，用于理解、审计和分析智能体的行为策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在复杂任务中表现出色，但其行为难以理解、解释和分析。现有评估方法主要关注任务成功率和执行轨迹，缺乏对智能体所采用策略的深入洞察。

Method: ATLAS结合轨迹抽象（trace abstraction）与自动机学习（automata learning），从智能体-环境交互轨迹中推断有限状态模型，捕捉高层行为策略，并支持符号模型的知识迁移（从大模型到小型语言模型）。

Result: 在LLM驱动的渗透测试智能体轨迹上验证，所学的行为模型能暴露难以从原始轨迹中识别的攻击漏洞机器的高层策略；在12台漏洞机器的案例研究中，通过模型变换可生成简洁的行为解释；并展示了模型驱动的知识迁移效果。

Conclusion: ATLAS展示了模型驱动工程的新机遇：将智能体轨迹转化为显式行为模型，使原本不可知的AI智能体能够被系统性地理解和分析，支持可解释性、引导式探索、审计等应用。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used for complex tasks such as software testing and cybersecurity assessment. While these agents demonstrate impressive capabilities, their behavior is difficult to understand, explain, and analyze. Existing evaluations focus mainly on task success and execution traces, offering limited insight into the strategies employed by the agent. We present ATLAS (Automata Learning for Agent Trajectory Analysis and Strategy Discovery), an approach for recovering interpretable behavioral models from agent trajectories. ATLAS combines trace abstraction with automata learning to infer finite-state models that capture observed agent-environment interaction strategies. These models provide human-interpretable insights and support automated analyses of recurring behaviors, decision points, successful task-completion paths, and failure loops. As a proof of concept, we apply ATLAS to trajectories generated by an LLM-based penetration-testing agent. The resulting models expose high-level behavioral strategies for exploiting vulnerable machines that are difficult to identify from raw execution traces alone. We discuss how learned behavioral models can support explainability, model-guided exploration, auditing, and analysis of agentic systems. We further demonstrate symbolic model-based knowledge transfer from powerful frontier models to compact language models. In addition, we show how model transformations can derive concise explanations of agent behavior in a penetration-testing case study comprising 12 vulnerable machines. ATLAS highlights a new opportunity for model-driven engineering: transforming agent trajectories into explicit behavioral models that enable systematic understanding and analysis of otherwise opaque AI agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Towards Scaling Qualitative Analysis of Video Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13594)
*Shiyi He*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了QualiVision，一个设计探针，探索交互式、电子表格支持的工作空间如何支持基于视频的定性分析，集成视频、转录文本、编码流、初步报告、启发式可视化和AI支持，以帮助研究者在分析演进过程中保留证据、比较解释并进行迭代反思性意义建构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着研究规模的增长，扩展定性视频分析变得困难，现有工具难以支持大规模研究中的证据保留、解释比较和迭代反思性分析。

Method: 通过设计探针（design probe）的方式构建QualiVision，一个整合视频、转录文本、编码流、初步报告、启发式可视化和AI支持的工作空间原型，用于探索其在视频定性分析中的支持效果。

Result: 论文呈现了QualiVision作为设计探针的构建与初步探索，旨在为视频定性分析提供综合性的工具支持（具体实证结果在摘要中未详细展开）。

Conclusion: QualiVision作为一个设计探针，展示了整合多种功能（视频、转录、编码、可视化、AI）的电子表格式工作空间有可能帮助研究者在视频定性分析中更好地保留证据、比较解释，并支持随分析演进而持续进行的迭代反思性意义建构。

Abstract: Scaling qualitative video analysis is difficult as studies grow. This paper presents QualiVision, a design probe examining how an interactive, spreadsheet-backed workspace can support video-based qualitative analysis. By integrating video, transcripts, coding streams, preliminary reports, heuristic visualization and AI support, QualiVision aims to help researchers preserve evidence, compare interpretations, and conduct iterative, reflexive sensemaking as their analysis evolves.

</details>


### [187] [Omni-LiveAvatar: Minute-Level Real-Time Streaming Joint Audio-Visual Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13602)
*Lunjie Zhu,Xingtong Ge,Fangyu Lin,Yi Zhang,Zhening Liu,Mengfei Li,Yumeng Zhang,Guanglu Song,Yu Liu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: Omni-LiveAvatar是首个支持分钟级、实时流式联合音视频化身生成的框架，通过渐进式自回归蒸馏、同步音视频长短期记忆和层级滚动提示规划三个核心组件，实现从大规模双向扩散模型到少步自回归生成器的高效转换，在保持高质量的同时实现33倍速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合音视频生成模型依赖双向注意力和多步去噪，仅能生成短视频片段，无法满足实时交互和长时间生成的需求，亟需一种能支持分钟级实时流式生成的新框架。

Method: 提出三个关键技术：(1)渐进式自回归蒸馏管道，无需辅助稳定机制即可将大型双向联合音视频扩散模型转化为少步自回归生成器；(2)同步音视频长短期记忆机制，在有限内存预算下保持全局一致性；(3)层级滚动提示规划策略，实现连贯的语义演化和平滑的提示转换。

Result: 在单张NVIDIA H200 GPU上相比教师模型LTX-2实现33倍生成加速，在视觉质量、音频质量、跨模态同步和人类保真度方面均优于加速基线方法。

Conclusion: Omni-LiveAvatar成功实现了分钟级、高质量、实时同步的音视频化身生成，为沉浸式和交互式数字人生成提供了可行的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Joint audio-video generative models serve as foundation for immersive and interactive digital-human generation. Nevertheless, most existing models rely on bidirectional attention and multi-step denoising and can generate only short clips, making them unsuitable for real-time interaction over extended durations. We present Omni-LiveAvatar, the first framework for minute-level, real-time streaming joint audio-video avatar generation. Specifically, we propose (1) a progressive autoregressive distillation pipeline that transfers a large bidirectional joint audio-video diffusion model into a few-step autoregressive generator without auxiliary stabilization mechanisms; (2) a synchronized audio-video long-short-term memory that preserves global consistency under a bounded memory budget; and (3) a hierarchical rolling prompt planning strategy that enables coherent semantic evolution and seamless prompt transitions. Extensive experiments show that Omni-LiveAvatar generates high-quality, synchronized minute-level avatars in real time. In terms of speed, it achieves a 33$\times$ generation speedup over its teacher, LTX-2, on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU; in terms of generation quality, it outperforms accelerated baselines across visual quality, audio quality, cross-modal synchronization, and human fidelity. Our code is available at https://github.com/Aoko955/Omni-LiveAvatar.

</details>


### [188] [AlignFace: Human-Aligned Face Similarity Metric with Interpretable Concept Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14130)
*Ying Huang,Wencan Zhang,Brian Y. Lim*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出AlignFace，一个基于认知心理学原理的可解释人脸相似度度量方法，通过引入FACETS数据集和ante-hoc建模（VLM编码、门控交叉注意力、概念瓶颈建模、神经GAM），显著提升了与不同人类亚群体感知的对齐度，弥补了现有感知度量缺乏认知对齐和忽视人群差异的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉人脸感知评估度量（如深度学习感知损失）缺乏认知心理学对齐，依赖隐式和虚假的相关性，并假设"普遍观察者"，忽略了不同人类群体对面部感知的固有差异，导致对利益相关者的评估不准确，并对生成模型的调试产生误导。

Method: 1) 引入FACETS数据集；2) 提出AlignFace度量，通过ante-hoc建模编码三条认知心理学原理：面部特征与构型属性的依赖、非线性心理物理响应缩放、内群体偏见；具体包括VLM编码成对面部图像与文本属性、门控交叉注意力提取属性特定差异表征、概念瓶颈建模约束可解释属性推理、神经广义加性模型建模非线性影响。

Result: 实验表明AlignFace在与人群体亚感知对齐方面显著优于基线度量，包括近期的领域无关学习感知度量。

Conclusion: 通过桥接学习表征与人类认知过程，本工作实现了更透明、更对齐的人脸图像感知评估度量，为生成式人脸内容评估提供了认知可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Computer vision models for generated facial content, such as face editing and privacy protection, increasingly affect people, requiring similarity metrics that serve as faithful proxies for human perception. While perceptual evaluation has progressed from signal-based heuristics to representation-based metrics, current approaches are limited to behavioral modeling without cognitive alignment. They rely on implicit and spurious relations while assuming a universal observer, failing to account for inherent variations across diverse human populations. This leads to inaccurate evaluative models of stakeholders and misleading guidance for generative model debugging. Rather than treating perception as a black box, we leverage scientific findings from cognitive psychology of human face similarity perception: dependence on facial featural and configural attributes, nonlinear psychophysical response scaling, and own-group biases. We introduce the FACETS dataset and propose AlignFace, an interpretable, human-aligned, face similarity metric that encodes these cognitive principles through ante-hoc modeling. It employs visual-language modeling (VLM) to encode paired face images and text-based attributes, gated cross-attention (CA) to extract attribute-specific facial difference representations, concept bottleneck modeling (CBM) to constrain reasoning via interpretable face attributes, and neural generalized additive model (GAM) to model their nonlinear influence. Experiments found AlignFace significantly improves alignment with human subpopulation perceptions compared to baseline metrics, including recent domain-free learned perceptual metrics. By bridging learned representations and human cognitive processes, this work enables more transparent and aligned perceptual evaluation metrics for face images.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileMem是一个面向设备端长期记忆的基准测试与框架，基于年尺度的移动体验数据，通过知识引导的合成管道构建连贯且时间一致的长时程轨迹，支持文本和多模态设置，涵盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断，推动AI智能体从信息检索走向经验智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代AI智能体正从回答孤立问题转向能够理解、记忆并持续学习的持久个人助理，需要长期记忆来积累和利用用户特定经验。然而现有基准在真实移动场景中不足，因为真实移动体验是异构、多模态、不断演变且高度个性化的。

Method: MobileMem采用知识引导的合成管道（knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline）从用户-应用会话中构建连贯且时间一致的长时程轨迹，提供互补的文本和多模态设置，覆盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断等任务。

Result: MobileMem使智能体能够记住过去、理解现在并适应未来，通过建模经验而非孤立事实，将记忆从信息检索推向经验智能，支持持续的个人学习。

Conclusion: 通过建模经验而非孤立事实，MobileMem将长期记忆研究从单纯的信息检索推向经验智能，为持续的个人化学习提供了新的基准和框架。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [190] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricForge，一种通过反思性演化从少量带真实标签的轨迹中自动生成评判准则文本的方法，用于替代人工编写的评分规则或微调的评判模型。该方法冻结优化后的可读准则，在无需环境访问的情况下用单次模型调用评估轨迹。实验表明，其核心优势在于提升评判的忠实性（降低错误通过率），而非单纯提升与真实奖励的一致度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体时，依赖第二个语言模型作为自动评判者，但现有方法（G-Eval式手写准则或微调评判模型权重）都倾向于给流畅但失败的轨迹打高分。作者希望将评判准则是从真实标注数据中归纳出来，从而grounded在真实结果上，提升评判的可信度。

Method: RubricForge通过反思性演化（reflective evolution）从少量真实标注轨迹中迭代优化评判准则，使其与真实环境奖励的一致性最大化。优化得到的准则以人类可读文本形式冻结，随后在单次模型调用中应用于留出的轨迹，无需访问环境。

Result: 在tau-bench（220次rollout中取173条标注轨迹）和WebShop（160条）上，使用同一冻结的7B模型作为智能体和评判者，RubricForge的主要增益是忠实性（faithfulness）而非原始一致性。与G-Eval相比，其统计显著性不高（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略有劣势（|err|差异-0.048，p=2x10^-4），但在tau-bench上错误通过率约为G-Eval的一半（0.115 vs 0.173），且无逆转案例；在WebShop上排序更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。作者强调对于无奖励的评判者，错误通过率（false-pass rate）才是部署相关的关键指标。

Conclusion: 对于无奖励信号环境下的智能体评估，评判者的核心质量指标应是错误通过率而非总体一致度，因为错误通过会导致有缺陷的智能体上线，而错误拒绝仅需重试。RubricForge通过从数据中归纳可读准则，有效减少错误通过，提供了比流量判分更安全的部署选择。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [191] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）进行了系统的逐层敏感性分析，发现层敏感性与深度强相关：早期和中层对专家掩码高度脆弱，而后期层（特别是35-39层）能容忍激进的低幅值专家掩码。通过仅掩码640个专家（占总数6.25%），500-prompt测试中仍保留419/500个Good+Similar输出，显著优于均匀掩码策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽然广泛采用，但各MoE层在模型压缩中的相对重要性尚未被充分刻画。理解层间敏感性差异对模型压缩和权重手术至关重要。

Method: 基于幅值的专家掩码方法，在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型进行多阶段（100/300/500 prompts）逐层敏感性分析，比较平坦全层掩码与聚焦晚期层的策略，并探索top-k路由宽度缩减（从8降至6）。
