Motivation: 在对抗性环境中，异构UAV/UGV群体需要应对传感器信息的不确定性、通信丢失、网络攻击等多重挑战，现有的协调方法难以同时保证任务执行的灵活性与硬件层面的物理安全，因此需要一个能够分离"解释-协调-执行"职责并兼顾安全性的架构。

Method: 该架构包含三个核心层次：1）顾问代理（Consultant agents）将多模态传感器输出转化为带时间边界的语义标记；2）监督代理（Supervisor agents）通过授权和策略管控来释放任务转换；3）数字协调层（TB-CSPN）通过守卫和同步约束协调层内的非确定性。此外，独立的模拟安全包络（analogue safety envelopes）对单个车辆的致动命令进行限制或否决，作为硬件层面的最终安全防线。

Result: 通过海岸监视案例研究，展示了该架构能够在运行不确定性下实现可靠、受控且物理安全的群体协调，提供可审计的决策路径，并在有限时间内整合异构证据。

Conclusion: 该架构通过解释层、协调层与执行层的清晰分离，在保证可审计性和可控非确定性的同时，利用独立的模拟安全包络有效应对包括网络破坏、欺骗、干扰和通信丢失在内的对抗性威胁，为异构群体在真实复杂环境中的可靠协调提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a coordination architecture for heterogeneous UAV/UGV swarms that synthesises mission actions from uncertain, multi-modal sensor evidence while preserving hardware-enforced safety at the actuation boundary. The approach combines radar, RF, acoustic, and visual observations with Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) orchestration to support incremental mission formation under partial and evolving information. Consultant agents transform sensor outputs into temporally bounded semantic tokens, while supervisor agents provide authorisation and policy-governed release of mission transitions. This separation between interpretation, coordination, and execution yields auditable decision paths, constrains non-determinism within the coordination layer through guards and synchronisation, and enables bounded-time integration of heterogeneous evidence. To improve resilience in contested environments, including cyber compromise, spoofing, jamming, and communication loss, the digital coordination layer is complemented by independent analogue safety envelopes that clamp or veto unsafe actuator commands issued to individual vehicles. A coastal-surveillance case study illustrates how the proposed architecture enables dependable, governed, and physically safe swarm coordination under operational uncertainty.

</details>


### [261] [Program-space Diffusion for Morphology-to-Transcriptomics Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14330)
*Ruyter Swann,Dorent Reuben,Racoceanu Daniel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将形态学到转录组的预测重新构建为转录程序空间中的条件生成任务，利用共识非负矩阵分解（cNMF）提取低维转录程序，并训练条件扩散模型从组织学图像生成程序激活值，从而利用协调的转录变异并降低条件生成任务的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学（ST）成本高且可扩展性有限，现有从组织学预测空间表达的方法大多在基因层面独立操作，未利用既有的转录组建模实践，且依赖异构的基因选择策略，导致方法间难以公平比较。

Method: 1) 使用共识非负矩阵分解（cNMF）从训练数据中提取捕获协调表达变异的低维转录程序；2) 训练条件扩散模型，以组织学图像为条件生成转录程序的激活值；3) 通过利用协调的转录变异，大幅降低条件生成任务的维度。

Result: 该方法能够在转录程序空间中实现形态学到转录组的预测，利用协调的转录变异而非独立预测基因，并降低了条件生成任务的维度。

Conclusion: 通过将预测任务重构为转录程序空间中的条件生成问题，本文为形态学到转录组的预测提供了一种更标准化且可公平比较的新范式。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables genome-wide gene expression profiling while preserving tissue architecture, but its cost and limited scalability remain major bottlenecks. This has motivated models that predict spatial expression directly from routine histology. Despite promising results, most existing approaches operate at the gene level without leveraging established transcriptomic modeling practices and rely on heterogeneous gene selection strategies, which complicates fair comparison across methods.
  We propose to reformulate morphology-to-transcriptomics prediction as conditional generation in transcriptional program space, thereby exploiting coordinated transcriptional variation instead of predicting genes independently. Using consensus non-negative matrix factorization (cNMF), we extract a low-dimensional set of transcriptional programs capturing coordinated expression variation in the training data, and train a conditional diffusion model to generate program activations from histology. This formulation exploits coordinated transcriptional variation and substantially lowers the dimensionality of the conditional generative task.

</details>


### [262] [Clearing the Fog: Towards Installing and Refining Proactive Exploration Capabilities in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14339)
*Zhizhao Guan,Chen Huang,Ziming Liu,Hongru Liang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng,Tat-Seng Chua,Anthony G Cohn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体的主动探索能力（即探索环境以获取信息来改善未来决策的能力），识别出阻碍该能力的两个根本瓶颈，并提出名为SAFARI的新方法。该方法包含两个组件：探索性数据构建（合成富含探索轨迹以缓解标准演示的后见之明偏差）和带对比信号引导的RL优化（利用对比轨迹对区分有效探索与冗余游荡）。实验证明其有效性，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在复杂环境中需要主动探索以获取信息改善决策，但现有方法缺乏这种能力。作者首先识别出两个根本瓶颈阻碍该能力，但摘要中未详细展开具体瓶颈内容，暗示存在事后偏差（hindsight bias）等问题。

Method: SAFARI包含两个核心组件：(1) 探索性数据构建——通过合成探索丰富的轨迹来缓解标准演示数据中存在的后见之明偏差；(2) 带对比信号引导的RL优化——利用对比轨迹对来区分有生产力的探索与冗余的游荡行为。

Result: 大量实验证明了SAFARI的有效性，并为主动探索的特征提供了洞见。

Conclusion: SAFARI成功提升了LLM智能体的主动探索能力，通过数据构建与对比RL优化相结合的方式有效解决了该领域的瓶颈问题，代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: We study proactive exploration in LLM agents, i.e., the ability to explore an environment to acquire information that improves future decision-making. In this regard, we first identify two fundamental bottlenecks that hinder this capability and then propose \ours, a novel method designed to instill and refine proactive exploration. Specifically, \ours\ consists of two components: (1) Exploratory Data Construction, which synthesizes exploration-rich trajectories to mitigate the hindsight bias of standard demonstrations; and (2) RL Optimization with Contrastive Signal Guidance, which leverages contrastive trajectory pairs to distinguish productive exploration from redundant wandering. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of \ours\ and provide insights into the characteristics of proactive exploration. Our code is available at: https://github.com/GuanZhizhao/SAFARI.

</details>


### [263] [ScienceFlow: A long-horizon agent for ML research, scientific discovery and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14354)
*Mingming Zhao,Jiqian Dong,Kangping Xu,Zadid Hasan,Chengrui Fan,Shan Jiang,Shuai Mao,Ting Lingya,Linyi Zou,Tailin Zhou,Yun Hin Chan,Wenkai Zhang,Zhanhong Zhou,Guowei Huang,Hongliang Li,Wenjing Cun,Zhitang Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Yanhui Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceFlow是一个端到端自动研究代理框架，通过可恢复的执行状态、可执行工作区段和证据感知的资源分配，解决长时程研究中的连续性问题，在MLE-bench上取得70.22%的SOTA成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动研究代理缺乏连续性机制、死胡同恢复能力和基于价值的计算资源分配，导致搜索效率低下、浪费计算资源并降低成功率。

Method: ScienceFlow将长时程研究组织为基于可执行工作区的研究片段，通过ESTRA机制在活跃状态与存档状态之间选择下一个锚点来决定继续或转向研究轨迹，并使用证据感知执行控制器根据资源可用性、剩余预算和已验证进度来分配计算资源。

Result: 在机器学习、科学建模和数学优化任务上评估，在24小时预算下MLE-bench完整集上取得70.22%的Any-Medal分数，较此前最优结果提升4.92个百分点。

Conclusion: 高效的状态管理、自适应探索和目标对齐的执行是扩展自主研究超越短时程交互的关键。

Abstract: Enabling LLM agents to sustain productive, stable, and goal-aligned research over extended horizons is a central challenge for autonomous machine learning and scientific discovery, as progress hinges on continuously managing evolving state, exploration decisions, and computational resources. Pioneering autoresearch agents, despite great success, still lack mechanisms for continuity, recovery from dead ends, and value-driven compute allocation, which inherently undermines overall search efficiency, wastes computational resources, and lowers the chance of ultimate success. To bridge this gap, we introduce ScienceFlow, an end-to-end autoresearch agent framework that organizes long-horizon research work into research segments grounded in executable workspaces. It represents research progress as recoverable executable states, enabling efficient exploration, revision, and execution. Transitions between research segments are governed by Executable-State Transition through Re-Anchoring (ESTRA), which selects either the live state or an archived state as the next anchor and determines whether to continue or redirect the research trajectory. An evidence-aware execution controller allocates resources to physical jobs based on resource availability, remaining budget, and validated progress. We evaluate ScienceFlow on tasks spanning machine learning, scientific modeling, and mathematical optimization. Results on diverse long-horizon benchmarks demonstrate its ability to sustain effective research processes, highlighted by a SOTA 70.22 percent Any-Medal score on the full MLE-bench within a 24-hour budget, outperforming prior reported results by 4.92 percentage points. The efficacy of ScienceFlow further demonstrates that efficient state management, adaptive exploration, and objective-aligned execution are critical for scaling autonomous research beyond short-horizon interactions.

</details>


### [264] [Disentangled Shared Representations Improve Morpho-Transcriptomic Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14355)
*Julian Ostermaier,Swann Ruyter,Reuben Dorent,Daniel Racoceanu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间转录组学（ST）中用于配对H&E图像和ST数据的多模态表示学习，重点探讨显式解耦共享与模态特定潜在成分是否能改善表示质量。通过在两个癌症队列中系统比较基于VAE和对比学习方法（每种均有标准和解耦变体），发现对比目标在下游探针任务上表现更优，而解耦变体在重构和探针指标上有所提升，但收益依赖于模型族、任务、方向和解耦强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多模态模型往往将所有模态压缩到共同潜在空间，但未明确分离共享与模态特定的变异来源，这可能限制下游任务的有效性。作者旨在探究显式解耦共享和私有潜在成分是否能改进配对H&E与ST数据的多模态表示学习。

Method: 比较两类多模态方法：基于VAE的方法和基于对比学习的方法，每种方法均构建标准变体和显式解耦变体（分离共享与私有成分）。在两个癌症队列中，采用匹配的实验条件进行对比，并通过三种评估方式衡量：跨模态重构、下游探针任务和跨模态探针迁移。

Result: 实验呈现两个主要趋势：1）对比学习目标在下游探针性能上优于VAE模型；2）解耦变体在所选重构和探针指标上有所改进，但增益的幅度取决于模型族、具体任务、迁移方向和解耦强度。

Conclusion: 显式分解共享与模态特定信息可以改善空间转录组学的多模态表示学习，该工作同时提供了一个有用的评估框架，可为未来的基础模型研究提供参考。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables the simultaneous profiling of gene expression and tissue morphology, creating an opportunity to learn multimodal representations capturing shared morpho-transcriptomic structure. However, standard multimodal models often compress modalities into a common latent space without explicitly separating shared and modality-specific sources of variation, which may limit downstream utility. We investigate whether explicit disentanglement of shared and private latent components improves multimodal representation learning for paired Hematoxylin \& Eosin (H\&E) and ST data. We compare VAE-based and contrastive approaches, each in standard and disentangled variants, across two cancer cohorts under matched experimental conditions. Representations are evaluated using cross-modal reconstruction, downstream probing and cross-modal probe transfer. The experiments suggest two main trends. First, contrastive objectives yield higher downstream probing performance than VAE-based models. Second, disentangled variants improve the selected reconstruction and probing metrics, although the gains depend on the model family, task, direction, and disentanglement strength. Overall, our results suggest that explicitly factorizing shared and modality-specific information can improve multimodal representation learning for spatial transcriptomics and provides a useful evaluation framework for future foundation models.

</details>


### [265] [Designing Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14359)
*Keya Patel,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Fattah,Deepak Kanneganti,Aneesh Krishna,Mufti Mahmud,Monowar Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SFLaaS，一个面向联邦学习即服务(FLaaS)场景的碳约束神经架构搜索(NAS)框架，通过需求驱动的搜索空间、消费者级碳可行性评估和可持续调度策略，在硬碳约束下保持联邦训练的可行性与参与覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLaaS消费者的可持续性约束给碳可行的联邦训练带来重大挑战，这些约束常导致在硬碳约束下出现不可行的消费者参与和联邦训练不稳定。

Method: 1) 需求驱动的搜索空间：将消费者可持续性画像转化为联邦执行前的可行架构区域；2) 消费者级碳可行性评估机制：在动态碳条件下评估候选架构；3) 可持续消费者调度策略：自适应选择可行消费者并分配本地工作负载以保持参与和统计数据覆盖；4) 进化搜索策略：在硬碳约束下联合优化预测性能、消费者可行性和参与覆盖。

Result: 在真实数据集和模拟环境中进行的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SFLaaS通过将碳约束纳入架构搜索和消费者调度的全流程，实现了在硬碳约束下兼顾预测性能、消费者可行性与参与覆盖的可持续联邦学习。

Abstract: The sustainability constraints of FLaaS consumers pose significant challenges to maintaining carbon-feasible federated training in FLaaS environments. These constraints often lead to infeasible consumer participation and unstable federated training under hard carbon constraints. We propose a Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service (SFLaaS), a carbon- constrained Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for heteroge- neous sustainable constraints. We introduce a requirement-driven search space that transforms consumer sustainability profiles into a feasible architecture region before federated execution. We develop a consumer-level carbon feasibility estimation mechanism to evaluate candidate architectures under dynamic carbon conditions. We propose a sustainable con- sumer scheduling strategy that adaptively selects feasible consumers and allocates local workloads to preserve consumer participation and statistical data coverage. An evolutionary search strategy jointly optimised for predictive performance, consumer feasibility, and participation coverage under hard carbon constraints. Experiments on real-world datasets and a simulated environment demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [266] [AgentRewind: Recoverable Execution for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14380)
*Yu Zhuang,Kefei Chen,Yitong Duan,Shuxin Zheng,Jian Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentRewind是一个运行时恢复框架，通过记录代理上下文和受控环境对齐的检查点，让LLM代理在长时执行中出错后能回滚到早期状态并恢复执行，同时构建了MettleBench基准来评估长时工程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间执行中早期发生的错误会通过代理上下文和环境状态传播，且难以通过后续动作逆转。现有方法主要通过计划细化和安全检查来减少错误，但在错误发生后缺乏恢复支持。

Method: 提出AgentRewind框架，记录代理上下文和受控环境的对齐检查点，使代理能回退到较早状态，并利用先前尝试的信息恢复执行。同时构建MettleBench基准，用于评估长时工程任务的任务完成度和部分进展。

Result: 在多种任务、多个模型、执行策略和代理框架上的实验表明，AgentRewind在任务成功率和平均检查表进展上均优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 通过在错误发生后提供回滚和恢复能力，AgentRewind有效提升了长时执行场景下LLM代理的任务成功率，弥补了现有方法在错误恢复方面的不足。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks require LLM agents to interact with their environments over long execution horizons. Errors that occur early in execution may propagate through both the agent context and environment state, and their effects may be difficult to reverse through subsequent actions. Existing methods mainly seek to reduce such errors through plan refinement and safety checks but provide little support after errors occur. To enable recovery during long-horizon execution, we present AgentRewind, a runtime recovery framework that records aligned checkpoints of the agent context and controlled environment, allowing agents to return to an earlier state and resume execution with information from previous attempts. We also construct MettleBench, a benchmark for evaluating task completion and partial progress on long-horizon engineering assignments containing a series of related requirements. Experiments across tasks, multiple models, execution strategies, and agent harnesses show that AgentRewind improves task success rate and average checklist progress over the compared baselines.

</details>


### [267] [Tripwire: Triggering Aligned Refusal via Statistically Certified Safety Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14392)
*Wei Zhao,Zhe Li,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tripwire，一种无需训练的防御方法，通过逐神经元假设检验（FDR控制）识别安全特异性神经元，并用触发式钳制将选中的神经元保持在有害条件均值激活状态，从而注入内部有害输入信号触发模型的拒绝行为，在不显著牺牲模型实用性的情况下有效防御越狱攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经元/路径层面的LLM越狱防御方法存在两大缺陷：一是抑制有毒神经元会因有害语义在网络中分布广泛而迫使干预足迹过大，损害模型实用性；二是基于外部分类器识别安全神经元的方法同样会损害对模型实用性重要的神经元。此外，现有方法始终开启，即使没有攻击也会扰动良性请求。

Method: Tripwire首先通过FDR控制的逐神经元假设检验结合实用性特异性过滤器来识别安全特异性神经元；然后采用触发式钳制（trigger-style clamp）将选中的神经元保持在有害条件均值激活水平，注入内部有害输入信号以触发对齐学习过程中的拒绝行为；最后通过两种可证明等价的部署模式实现：检测器门控的推理时干预和离线偏置补丁权重编辑。

Result: 在四个安全对齐的LLM和四种代表性攻击上，Tripwire将平均攻击成功率降至至多2.0%，同时在MT-Bench上的实用性下降仅为0.5%至5.3%，是所有防御方法中最低的。

Conclusion: Tripwire通过精准识别安全特异性神经元并采用触发式干预而非抑制有毒语义的方式，在防御有效性和模型实用性之间取得了最优平衡，同时提供了无需训练的轻量级部署方案。

Abstract: Neuron- and path-level interventions offer the finest-grained route to defending large language models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, yet existing methods fall short of this promise, i.e., they often compromise model utility significantly. Specifically, one line of work suppresses toxic neurons to erase harmful semantics, but since such semantics are distributed across the network, blocking every pathway forces a large intervention footprint. An alternative line of research focus on identify safety neurons using external classifiers. While promising, the existing approaches suffer from compromising neurons that are important for the model utility as well. Moreover, both approaches remain always on and thus perturb every benign request even when no attack is present. To address these limitations, we present \ours{}, a training-free defense that first identifies safety-specific neurons through per-neuron hypothesis tests under false-discovery-rate control together with a utility-specificity filter. Based on this identification, a trigger-style clamp holds the selected neurons at their harmful-conditional mean activations, injecting an internal harmful-input signal that triggers the refusal behavior learned during alignment. The clamp is then realized by two provably equivalent deployment modes, namely a detector-gated inference-time intervention and an offline bias-patch weight edit. Extensive experiments across four safety-aligned LLMs and four representative attacks demonstrate that \ours{} reduces the average attack success rate to at most 2.0\% while incurring a utility drop of only 0.5\% to 5.3\% on MT-Bench, the smallest among all defenses. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Tripwire-65C4.

</details>


### [268] [The Past and Future of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14407)
*Ross D. King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI科学家（能够自动化科学研究的机器）的过去与未来。文章回顾了Adam和Eve等早期系统，指出核心挑战已从单个组件自动化转向系统集成，并讨论了AI科学家对科学变革的潜力以及Nobel Turing Challenge的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在系统性地梳理AI科学家领域的发展历程，指出当前的核心瓶颈已不再是科学中单个组件的自动化（这些已可实现），而是如何将这些组件（神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、仿真、实验设计、机器人技术等）整合为统一的科学智能体系统。

Method: 本文采用综述（survey）方法，回顾了AI科学家的发展历史——从首个通过假设形成和物理实验循环做出新颖科学发现的Adam，到建立现代自驱动实验室架构的Eve，再到当前基于基础模型、自主智能体和实验室机器人构建的更通用系统，并展望未来发展方向。

Result: 文章指出AI科学家有潜力使科学更快、更便宜、更系统化、更可复现，能够探索人类单独难以处理的复杂系统，并允许多个AI科学家协同解决单一问题。Nobel Turing Challenge设定了到2050年开发能自动化诺贝尔级发现的AI系统的目标，目前进展超前于计划。

Conclusion: AI科学家的发展正在加速，其成功将创造一种新的科学形式并改变世界。领域的前进方向在于解决系统集成这一核心问题，而非单个组件的能力提升。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [269] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出optstop，一个基于精度的自适应停止框架，将LLM评估视为序贯测量问题，在不确定性高的地方继续采样，在估计精确或稳定处停止。通过分层贝叶斯推断支持二值、有序和连续结果，无需校准题库。在200项10轮次的评估中，减少了57%-97%的计划试验，且结论与完整运行等效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估使用固定的采样预算，对每个测试项进行相同次数的测试，即使在估计已足够精确后仍继续采样，导致大量计算资源浪费。

Method: 基于分层贝叶斯推断的精密驱动自适应停止框架。核心思想：将评估建模为序贯测量问题——在不确定性高的地方继续采样，在估计精确或稳定时停止。支持二值、有序和连续结果类型；每个基准项都保持采样资格，无需校准题库；可在线运行或事后回放；包含安全机制，在性能接近零时（罕见成功最重要）更谨慎地采样。

Result: 在200项、10 epoch的示例评估中，跨9个验证设置消除了57%-97%的计划试验，同时整体结论与完整运行等效。

Conclusion: LLM评估的计算资源可以根据不确定性而非固定重复次数来分配，节省幅度取决于评估设计的具体情况。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [270] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI辅助AI研发形成反馈回路时可能产生的智能爆炸动力学。作者表明奇异增长（垂直渐近线）比近期经济学启发模型预期更难实现，并发现一类被忽视的"超指数但无垂直渐近线"的增长速率。关键发现是生成时间（完成反馈回路的时间）是被忽视的核心参数——除非生成时间快速趋近于零，否则不可能出现奇异增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI被越来越广泛地用于辅助AI研发，这一自我改进的反馈回路可能引发智能爆炸。作者希望从数学上严格探究最具爆炸性的可能性，理解驱动这一动力学的关键因素，并纠正近期经济学启发式模型中可能存在的过度乐观预期。

Method: 作者采用数学建模与理论分析的方法，探讨智能爆炸反馈回路的动力学。重点分析了生成时间（完成一轮反馈所需时间）这一参数在决定爆炸行为中的核心作用，并对奇异增长（垂直渐近线）与超指数增长（无垂直渐近线）进行了数学区分和严格分析。

Result: 研究表明：（1）奇异增长比经济学启发模型所预示的更难实现；（2）存在一类重要的被忽视的增长速率——比指数快但不趋向垂直渐近线；（3）生成时间是决定智能爆炸行为的关键参数，若无法快速趋近于零，则奇异增长不可能发生。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：智能爆炸的动力学远比此前所认为的更为微妙，生成时间而非其他参数是决定爆炸形态的核心因素。这为理解AI自我改进的可能边界提供了更精确的数学框架，也暗示了对智能爆炸的悲观（或乐观）预期需要修正。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [271] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE-Bench，一个基于模拟器的自进化智能体基准测试，包含144个跨六个物理领域的源到目标适配对，用于评估智能体在环境条件变化后通过代码进化和诊断性沙箱反馈恢复和适应的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体的评估通常在固定执行条件下进行优化，没有测试环境条件变化后的恢复能力。这导致缺乏对智能体在物理条件突变后适应能力的评测。

Method: 构建PACE-Bench基准，包含144个源到目标适配对，覆盖六个物理领域。每个适配对将源环境与具有相同目标和接口的突变目标环境关联，智能体需要在有限的尝试预算内，利用诊断性沙箱反馈，将源代码迭代适配成能在目标环境中工作的设计。对比了来自四个范式的十种自进化方法。

Result: 基准远未饱和：Reflexion + Qwen3-14B仅在全基准对中成功35.9%，GPT-5.5在完整预算下解决静态子集66.7%。模拟器接地反思比未经验证的自修订更可靠；记忆将智能体锚定在早期设计上；广泛树搜索探索但不收敛。揭示精确物理变化并不会提高性能上限。

Conclusion: 研究指出机制重新设计而非参数推断是核心瓶颈，PACE-Bench为评估自进化智能体在环境变化后的适应能力提供了重要测试平台。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [272] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wyvern是一个多智能体框架，用于自动生成有依据的多模态技术报告，集成了图像、表格、文本和引用，并通过声明自动修订阶段确保内容可落地。人类评估显示其图表信息量在87%情况下优于基线，报告实用性在63%-100%情况下优于三种替代方法；自动评估显示其引用召回率提升2.3倍、精确率提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动的创新时代知识增长加速，人类难以跟上步伐。虽然生成模型被广泛用于内容合成，但往往缺乏信息落地（grounding），导致生成内容可能不准确或缺乏实证支持。

Method: 提出了Wyvern多智能体框架，能够生成整合图像、表格、文本及参考文献的统一多模态报告。特别设计了一个声明自动修订阶段来增强内容的落地性，确保每个声明都有可靠来源支撑。

Result: 人类评估中，Wyvern的图表信息量在87%案例中被认为优于近期基线；报告实用性与三种替代方法相比在63%-100%的实例中获得更高评分。自动评估显示Wyvern的引用召回率提升2.3倍、引用精确率提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern通过多智能体协作和声明自动修订机制，能够在生成多模态技术报告的同时确保内容的落地性和引用可靠性，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [273] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SheetCompass框架，通过图结构和记忆机制增强LLM对复杂电子表格的推理能力，解决现有方法丢失多维结构信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的电子表格包含隐式的跨表关联、细粒度的列依赖和复杂的空间布局，但现有方法将多维结构扁平化为顺序字符串，丢失了工作簿内部边界和跨工作表语义，导致LLM无法利用人类专家常用的全局空间上下文。

Method: SheetCompass是一个图引导和记忆驱动的智能体框架，显式建模工作表内外的结构关系，同时在记忆中维护任务相关信息，使智能体能够在复杂工作簿上更有效地推理。

Result: 论文摘要未直接给出具体实验结果，但宣称该方法使LLM在复杂工作簿上的推理更为有效。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模电子表格的结构关系和记忆机制，可以显著提升LLM在电子表格推理与自动化任务中的表现。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [274] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于移位感知的双编码器迁移学习框架，用于短时PM2.5预报。该框架结合了源域（美国10个监测站）的预训练知识和目标域（台湾77个站点）的特定表征学习，在时间序列训练-验证-测试协议下取得了优于传统迁移学习基线的性能（MSE=21.6575, R²=0.8739），消融实验和SHAP分析验证了目标域特定分支和近期PM2.5观测及气象变量的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时PM2.5预报在目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性存在差异时仍然困难。仅依赖本地数据训练的模型可能无法捕捉复杂的时序动态，而直接迁移学习可能导致负迁移。因此需要一种能够兼顾源域知识利用和目标域特定学习的框架。

Method: 提出移位感知双编码器迁移框架：1) 源编码器使用美国10个监测站的小时观测数据预训练；2) 框架迁移到台湾时引入目标域特定分支（台湾特定表征学习分支）；3) 在77个台湾站点上采用按时间顺序划分的训练-验证-测试协议进行评估，并与四个主要基线比较；4) 进行消融实验分析各组件贡献；5) 使用SHAP分析进行模型可解释性。

Result: 冻结源编码器的双编码器模型在四个基线中表现最佳（MSE=21.8960, MAE=3.1597, R²=0.8725），MSE较TL-v1降低约7.1%，较TL-v2降低4.1%。消融实验显示移除台湾特定分支导致性能下降最大。允许源编码器适应目标域后获得最佳整体结果（MSE=21.6575, MAE=3.1383, R²=0.8739）。SHAP分析表明预测主要由近期PM2.5观测和与污染物传输及扩散相关的气象变量驱动。

Conclusion: 源域知识在保留目标域特定信息且迁移表征在目标域监督下允许适应时最为有效。该框架成功解决了数据有限和域差异条件下的短时PM2.5预报问题，验证了双编码器架构在负迁移缓解方面的有效性。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [275] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为Twin的测试时世界模型推理系统，让前沿编码智能体为ARC-AGI-3等持续学习任务编写可执行的世界模型。系统通过模拟和交互推断游戏规则与目标，使用回放验证和反例修复机制，在183个关卡中清关179个（97.8%），并在158个关卡中比人类更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法为每个任务手工设计世界模型，无法适应规则和目标隐藏的网格游戏。需要一个能从模拟和交互中自动构建世界模型的通用系统。

Method: 系统采用测试时世界模型推理：编码智能体编写可执行世界模型，通过回放验证确保程序能重现所有先前观测到的游戏转换。预测与实际结果不匹配时产生反例用于修复模型。世界模型构建完毕后，系统在孪生世界模型中进行推理和动作执行。

Result: Twin清除了183个关卡中的179个（97.8%），在179个清关卡中有158个比人类更高效（88.3%）。在156个关卡中（87.2%）在获得任何奖励前推断出目标，其余关卡通过搜索自动发现目标。基础模型直接得分为7.8%，现成框架提升至61.1%，Twin提升至93.3%。

Conclusion: 构建可用的世界模型比预期的更简单，而更难的问题在于推断正确的目标。该结果表明自动世界模型推理是持续学习任务的可行路径。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [276] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究道德AI偏好elicitation管线中三个关键决策（特征范围、选民抽样、问题框架）如何影响生成的道德偏好，通过N=809的两阶段实验在三个部署场景中证明每个选择都会显著改变偏好结果，呼吁对这些规范性的选择进行审计和披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地做出道德决策，偏好elicitation被用作对齐方法，但开发者在投票前做出的三个关键选择（特征范围、选民抽样、问题框架）往往不透明、未文档化，被当作技术细节而非规范性决策处理，这可能影响最终道德偏好的公正性。

Method: 在两阶段研究（N=809）中，针对三个部署场景（AI肾分配、AI替代缺勤员工、生成式AI描绘逝者），系统性地检验了道德AI偏好elicitation管线的三个阶段：1）特征范围是否跨场景可迁移；2）不同政治意识形态的偏好差异；3）问题措辞对意识形态差距的影响及道德基础关联的变化。

Result: 研究发现：1）道德相关特征在不同场景间不转移，特征模式不应假设可跨域泛化；2）约三分之一的特征存在政治意识形态偏好差异，部分差异方向反转，选民池构成会影响聚合偏好；3）问题措辞可将意识形态差距缩小或扩大达一个完整量表点，框架条件还改变道德基础与判断的关联方式。

Conclusion: 基于投票的对齐无法仅靠聚合实现公平或透明的AI。道德偏好elicitation管线的每一阶段都应进行审计并对外披露，因为这些选择本质上是规范性决策而非纯技术性细节。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


### [277] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型应用中"会话交接"（session handover）的方法论和理论性质。将交接形式化为任务相关上下文学习（ICL）状态的转移，区分精确恢复早期材料与保留目标分布。在外生性条件下，预测等价性刻画了最粗糙的确定性充分交接，并给出了固定长度的比特需求。提出三部分记录方案，并在高斯线性回归和非参数回归中给出了上下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用中任务常因上下文达到输入限制、应用重启或移交其他代理而需要跨会话继续。此时必须决定将早期会话的哪些信息传递给新会话，但缺乏理论框架来指导这一决策——哪些信息必须保留，记忆需求如何依赖于后续任务。

Method: 将交接形式化为任务相关ICL状态转移；提出外生性条件下预测等价性刻画最粗糙确定性充分交接；分析记忆约束、写入者和继续流程的影响；量化在得知下游查询之前写入的成本；提出三部分记录（精确存储决策与约束、任务合理性统计量、无法被统计量保留的原始观测）；在Gaussian线性回归和非参数回归中给出理论界限。

Result: Gaussian线性回归得到精确的有限维交接和有限比特扰动界；非参数回归给出将记忆与平方预测误差相关联的上下界；预测等价性给出固定长度的比特需求。

Conclusion: 提供了关于交接必须保留什么、记忆需求如何依赖于继续任务的理论和方法，为LLM应用中的会话交接设计提供了正式的理论基础。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [Continual Evolution Strategies in Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13600)
*Nicola Pitzalis,Eleni Nisioti,Antonio Carta,Davide Bacciu,Andrea Cossu*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了进化策略（ES）在持续控制任务中的表现，发现朴素的ES在顺序MuJoCo运动任务上存在严重的灾难性遗忘，而回放（replay）机制能显著改善保留能力并可能产生正向迁移，但更大的回放预算会降低可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续控制（continual control）要求智能体在不断变化的任务中适应新任务而不遗忘旧任务，这是一个实际且具有挑战性的问题。作者希望探究ES这一类黑盒优化方法在持续控制场景下的表现，特别是其遗忘行为和缓解机制。

Method: 在顺序MuJoCo运动任务上系统地评估了三种设置：朴素的ES（无记忆机制）、带回放（replay）的ES，并对比不同的回放预算大小，观察其对保留能力（retention）和可塑性（plasticity）的影响。

Result: 1）朴素的ES在持续控制中遭受严重的灾难性遗忘；2）回放机制大幅提升了任务保留能力，并能诱导正向迁移；3）更大的回放预算虽然进一步提升保留，但会降低模型的可塑性。

Conclusion: ES可以支持持续控制中的持续适应，回放是缓解遗忘的有效机制，但需要在保留能力与可塑性之间进行权衡。

Abstract: We study Evolution Strategies (ES) for continual control, where agents must adapt to changing tasks without forgetting previous ones. On sequential MuJoCo locomotion tasks, naive ES suffers from severe catastrophic forgetting. Replay substantially improves retention and can induce positive transfer, while larger replay budgets reduce plasticity. Overall, these results show that ES can support continual adaptation in control and that replay is an effective mechanism for mitigating forgetting.

</details>


### [279] [Reducing ANN-SNN Conversion Error via Residual Membrane Potential Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13952)
*Zirui Chen,Zihan Huang,Tong Bu,Jianhao Ding,Yiting Dong,Zhaofei Yu*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合动态初始电位调整和特征增强的ANN-SNN转换策略，通过引入正则化损失L_RMPD和SCR-Conv2d竞争细化层，解决了低时间步长下转换SNN的准确性下降、推理延迟和累积量化误差问题，在CIFAR和ImageNet上验证了显著的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ANN-SNN转换方案在小时间步长下存在严重的精度损失、较大的推理延迟和累积量化误差，即使在大时间步长下性能接近，也限制了SNN在神经形态芯片上的实际部署。

Method: 1) 分析传统转换流程在残余膜电位统计上的缺陷；2) 提出结合动态初始电位调谐和特征增强的转换策略；3) 引入正则化损失L_RMPD调整IF神经元的初始电位，缓解边界聚合导致的系统性截断偏差；4) 构建带分组卷积的SCR-Conv2d竞争细化层，增强特征判别力、消除冗余脉冲并稳定编码；5) 与QCFS基线集成。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet上，在T=2,4,8的时间步长下取得显著的精度提升，计算开销可忽略不计，并泛化到ReLU CNN、ANN Transformer和多阈值SNN变体。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种有效的转换范式，有助于促进SNN在神经形态芯片上的实际部署。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) serve as core architectures for neuromorphic computing thanks to event-driven operation and ultra-low power consumption. Direct SNN training is hindered by non-differentiable spikes that induce vanishing gradients and unstable optimization. ANN-SNN conversion circumvents such issues by reusing well-trained ANN weights for low-latency, energy-efficient inference. Nevertheless, existing conversion schemes suffer from severe accuracy drops at small timesteps, large inference delays and cumulative quantization errors, even with marginal performance loss at large $T$. To address these limitations, we first analyze flaws of conventional conversion pipelines from residual membrane potential statistics and propose a novel conversion strategy combining dynamic initial potential tuning and feature enhancement. We then introduce a regularization loss $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{RMPD}}$ to adapt initial potential of IF neurons and mitigate systematic truncation bias from boundary aggregation. A dedicated SCR-Conv2d competitive refinement layer with grouped convolution is further built to sharpen feature discrimination, eliminate redundant spikes and stabilize encoding under tiny time windows. Integrated with the state-of-the-art QCFS baseline, our approach delivers consistent low-latency performance gains and generalizes to ReLU CNNs, ANN Transformers, and multi-threshold SNN variants. Evaluations on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100 and ImageNet verify prominent accuracy improvements at $T=2,4,8$, with negligible extra computation overhead. This work offers an effective conversion paradigm to facilitate real-world SNN deployment on neuromorphic chips.

</details>


### [280] [Emergent Models: Intelligence from Tiny Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14019)
*Giacomo Bocchese,Nicola Giacobbo,Etienne Guichard,James Wiles,Akshaj Devireddy*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出"涌现模型"(Emergent Models, EMs)这一机器学习范式，将建模视为在简单动态系统（如元胞自动机）中涌现出能解决外部任务的计算行为，而非学习封闭形式的输入输出映射。作者从理论上证明了某些EMs具有"潜在通用性"（latent-universal），即固定更新规则和接口，仅通过变化潜在状态的初始条件即可实现任意部分可计算函数；实验上在离散和连续底物上验证了极简EM实例（数十到数百参数）能精确外推简单算术函数、支持控制行为和在线适应，同时揭示了若干局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习依赖可微的前馈映射和封闭形式的输入输出函数，限制了设计空间。作者希望探索一种更开放的范式——在简单但开放式的动态系统（如元胞自动机）中，通过进化搜索涌现出能解决任务的计算行为，从而拓宽机器学习的设计边界，并特别关注这类框架是否具有全局泛化（外推）能力。

Method: (1) 提出EMs框架：包含一个固定的局部更新规则、潜在状态空间、连接外部输入/输出的接口，以及进化搜索的训练方式；(2) 理论上证明某些EMs是latent-universal的——仅改变初始条件即可实现任何部分可计算函数；(3) 实证上构建了覆盖离散和连续底物的极简EM"动物园"，测试其在算术外推、控制行为和在线适应任务上的表现。

Result: 实验显示，极小的EM实例（数十到数百参数）能够：(a)在简单算术函数上实现精确外推；(b)支持控制行为；(c)支持在线适应。但同时也暴露出若干局限性。理论证明了latent-universal性质，但作者强调这是基础性工作，并非提出竞争性的架构。

Conclusion: 这项工作旨在拓宽机器学习的设计空间，超越可微前馈映射的范式。EMs作为一种新兴范式，具有全局泛化的潜力，但其当前形式并非面向性能竞争力的架构，而是为未来研究提供概念框架和理论基础的奠基性工作。

Abstract: Emergent Models (EMs) are a machine learning paradigm based on simple yet open-ended substrates, such as cellular automata, in which modeling is treated not as the learning of a closed-form input-output map but as the emergence, within simple dynamical systems, of computational behaviors that solve external tasks. Such substrates typically iterate a fixed local rule over a latent space for an adaptive number of steps, with an interface linking the latent state to external input/output signals. Training proceeds by evolutionary search. We hypothesize that some instances of this framework are biased toward global generalization: capturing the rule generating the data over its full domain, and therefore extrapolating beyond the training range. Theoretically, we prove that some EMs are latent-universal: with the update rule and interface held fixed, they can realize any partial computable function by varying only the initial condition of the latent state. Empirically, we study a zoo of minimal EM instantiations across discrete and continuous substrates, showing that local-recursive computation at a tiny scale (tens to hundreds of parameters) can extrapolate exactly on simple arithmetic functions, can support control behaviour and online adaptation, while still exposing several limitations. This work is foundational: it does not propose a competitive architecture, but a framework meant to widen the design space of machine learning beyond differentiable feed-forward maps.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Do AI chatbots find what experts would? Effects of model, user role, and sample size on study retrieval for medical questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13786)
*Qingfang Liu,Qiao Jin,Joe D. Menke,Thorsten Kahnt,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三个通用大语言模型聊天机器人（Claude Sonnet 5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、ChatGPT GPT-5.5）在回答临床问题时引用研究的质量。通过模拟患者、临床医生和循证研究者三种角色，对Cochrane系统评价中的20个临床问题进行720次查询，发现聊天机器人平均召回39.2%的Cochrane纳入研究，引用5.0%的排除研究，其中ChatGPT召回率最高（63.1%），且研究存在倾向于引用大样本量临床试验的偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于LLM聊天机器人临床应用的研究主要关注引文捏造问题，而忽略了检索到的研究的质量以及驱动研究选择的因素，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用三个通用LLM聊天机器人（Claude Sonnet 5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、ChatGPT GPT-5.5），针对来自2026年Cochrane系统评价第6和第7期的20个综述问题，在患者、临床医生和循证研究者三种角色下各自进行4次独立重复查询，共获得720个响应，并将引文与Cochrane综述的纳入和排除研究集进行基准比较。

Result: 平均每个响应检索到39.2%±29.8%的Cochrane纳入研究，引用5.0%±9.4%的排除研究。ChatGPT的召回率显著高于Claude和Gemini（63.1% vs 37.0% vs 17.3%）。研究者角色的召回率高于临床医生和患者角色。在控制发表年份、年引用量和开放获取状态后，样本量是唯一独立显著的检索预测因子（OR=1.80）。

Conclusion: LLM聊天机器人能够检索到专家评审员确定的部分研究，但其性能因模型和用户角色而异，且存在偏向于样本量较大的临床试验的选择偏倚。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) chatbots are increasingly used to answer clinical questions with citations to relevant clinical studies. Prior research has largely focused on citation fabrication, leaving a gap in evaluating the quality of retrieved studies and the factors driving their selection. In this study, we evaluated three general-purpose LLM chatbots: Claude Sonnet 5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and ChatGPT GPT-5.5. We prompted the models with clinical questions adapted from 20 review questions in Issues 6 and 7 of the 2026 Cochrane Database of Systematic Reviews, simulating patient, clinician, and evidence-synthesis researcher roles. Each chatbot was queried under each user role with four independent repetitions, yielding 720 responses. Each chatbot was asked to support its answers with primary clinical citations, which we benchmarked against the included and excluded study sets of the Cochrane reviews. On average, a chatbot response retrieved 39.2% $\pm$ 29.8% of Cochrane included studies, while citing 5.0% $\pm$ 9.4% of excluded studies. Recall of Cochrane included studies varied significantly by model and user role. ChatGPT achieved higher recall than Claude or Gemini (63.1% $\pm$ 29.5% vs. 37.0% $\pm$ 23.8% vs. 17.3% $\pm$ 13.1%; $p=2.0\times10^{-5}$). The researcher role yielded higher recall than the clinician or patient roles (42.8% $\pm$ 30.8% vs. 38.6% $\pm$ 28.9% vs. 36.1% $\pm$ 29.3%; $p=2.0\times10^{-5}$). Controlling for publication year, citations per year, and open-access status, sample size was the only independently significant predictor of retrieval (odds ratio 1.80 per 1-unit increase in log sample size, 95% CI 1.37-2.36, $p=2.34\times10^{-5}$). These findings suggest that while LLM chatbots can retrieve some studies identified by expert reviewers, their performance varies by model and user role, and they exhibit a bias toward clinical trials with larger sample sizes.

</details>


### [282] [AdsWorldEngine: A Self-Evolving Conversational Advertising Agent through Orchestrator and Tool Coevolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13833)
*Simiao Zuo,Chenhui Xu,Yimeng Jia,Qiang Lou,Jian Jiao,Denis Charles*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AdsWorldEngine是一个面向对话式广告的智能体框架，通过机会门控、编排器和评估器三模块协同工作，并引入迭代演员-工具训练和标签引导的判断建模，在离线和在线测试中均显著提升了广告投放效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式广告与传统查询式广告不同，需要从多轮对话中推断潜在商业意图，并判断广告是否有助于用户而非打扰。现有系统难以处理这种复杂场景下的广告投放决策。

Method: 提出AdsWorldEngine智能体框架，包含三个核心组件：机会门（Opportunity Gate）判断是否展示广告、编排器（Orchestrator）生成商业意图并调用广告工具构建top-3广告列表、评估器（Evaluator）对投放广告打分用于离线优化。关键在于迭代演员-工具训练流程：先用SFT和智能体强化学习训练编排器，再利用高/低回报轨迹构建偏好数据来训练工具，形成自我改进循环。此外引入标签引导判断建模，从人工标注中训练判断模型，通过思维链丰富标签、反思过滤不一致推理，并用成本敏感的GRPO变体优化二元判断。

Result: 离线实验中，多样性提升60%、相关性提升80%；在线A/B测试中，RPM提升22%、广告覆盖率提升74%。

Conclusion: AdsWorldEngine通过智能体框架与迭代自改进训练机制，有效解决了对话式广告中的意图推断与广告投放决策问题，显著优于现有生产系统，验证了actor-tool联合训练与判断建模方法的有效性。

Abstract: Conversational advertising aims to deliver useful ads within multi-turn assistant interactions. Unlike conventional query-based advertising, where the user's intent is often expressed in a short standalone query, conversational ads must infer latent commercial intent from the current user query, the assistant response, and dialogue history while also deciding whether an ad would be helpful rather than intrusive. We propose AdsWorldEngine, an agentic framework for conversational advertising. AdsWorldEngine uses an Opportunity Gate to determine whether ads should be shown, an Orchestrator to generate commercial intents, call advertising tools, and construct a top-3 ad slate, and an Evaluator to score delivered ads for offline optimization. The central contribution is an iterative actor-tool training procedure: we first train the Orchestrator with supervised fine-tuning and agentic reinforcement learning, then use high- and low-reward rollouts to construct preference data to train tools. This creates a self-improving loop in which the system learns not only how to use advertising tools, but also how to improve them from rewarded behavior. To support subjective production decisions, we introduce label grounded judgment modeling, which trains judgment models from human labels collected under explicit guidelines. It enriches labels with thinking traces, filters inconsistent rationales through reflection, and further optimizes binary judgments with a cost sensitive GRPO variant that preserves asymmetric reward gaps. Offline, AdsWorldEngine improves diversity by 60% and relevance by 80% over the current production ad delivery system. In an online A/B test, it increases RPM by 22% and ads coverage by 74%.

</details>


### [283] [Predicting Custom-Feed Returns for New Bluesky Posts: A Prospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13874)
*Yipeng Wang,Mohit Singhal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的冷启动路由任务：在Bluesky自定义信息流场景中，将新发布的公开帖文作为查询，对面板中可排序的 feeds 进行排序，预测哪些 feed 会返回该帖文。作者构建了一个持续演化的"先采集、后标注"基准数据集，涵盖5,000个监控 feed、1,786万条公开帖文及62.5万次有效 feed 轮询，并采用LambdaRank等方法进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的冷启动推荐主要针对新用户或新物品，而Bluesky自定义feeds创造了一种不同的场景：独立运营的feeds从共享公共流中过滤内容。在这种场景下，新发布的帖文是冷启动对象，feeds则是候选集。现有方法无法直接适用于这种"帖文到feed"的路由问题，因此需要新的任务定义和数据集来支撑研究。

Method: 作者将该问题形式化为冷启动路由任务：以新摄入的公开帖文为查询，对监测面板中所有可排序的feeds进行排序，预测每个feed后续是否会返回该帖文。构建了一个"先采集、后标注"的基准数据集，监控5,000个固定feed面板，标注在帖文发布后24小时内是否出现在某个feed的AppView Top-50结果中。实验采用两个不重叠的24小时测试折，每个折配有24小时训练窗口，中间间隔24小时的结果可用性窗口。评估条件限定在至少有一个正向标注且满足指标资格标准的测试帖文上。

Result: 实验使用了602,186个符合条件的测试帖文（占全部6,661,658个测试帖文的9.04%）。在两个测试折上，LambdaRank取得了最好的平均结果：capped Recall@10为0.7361，NDCG@10为0.6127，Hit@10为0.7749。

Conclusion: 该研究定义了一个新颖的冷启动路由任务，并提供了一个大规模、持续演化的基准数据集，验证了LambdaRank在该任务上的有效性。该工作为社交平台自定义feed场景下的内容分发与路由研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: The conventional approach to cold-start recommendation addresses new users or newly introduced items. Bluesky custom feeds create a different setting: independently operated feeds filter content from a shared public stream. In this setting, newly published posts are the cold-start objects, while the feeds serve as candidates. We propose a cold-start routing task in which a newly ingested public post is the query and all rankable feeds in the monitored panel are ranked according to whether each will subsequently return it. We build a still-evolving collect-first, label-later benchmark dataset. The collected dataset covers a fixed panel of 5,000 monitored feeds and contains 17.804 million public posts, 1.865 million observable post--feed return records, and 625,083 valid feed polls. The labels record whether a post is observed among a feed's AppView Top-50 results in at least one poll during the 24 hours after publication. The current experiments use two disjoint 24-hour test folds, each paired with a 24-hour training window and separated by a 24-hour outcome-availability gap. Evaluation is conditional on the 602,186 test posts that have at least one positive observed label and satisfy the metric eligibility criteria; these posts account for 9.04% of all 6,661,658 test posts. Across the two folds, LambdaRank achieves the best equal-fold mean values among the evaluated models: 0.7361 for capped Recall@10, 0.6127 for NDCG@10, and 0.7749 for Hit@10.

</details>


### [284] [How retriever redundancy and diversity impact RAG effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13956)
*Jonathan J Ross,Bevan Koopman,Anton van der Vegt,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究了RAG系统中检索文档集的冗余性与多样性对生成器答案正确性的影响，发现重复文档和LLM改写不显著提升性能，而多样化的文档（不同体裁）能提升17%-47%的答案正确性，且这种提升源于体裁多样性本身而非答案可获取性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于RAG中检索文档冗余性与多样性对生成器影响的研究结论混杂，且未控制关键混淆因素（如文档是否包含精确答案、参数化知识的作用）。本文旨在通过严格受控的实验厘清这些因素的真实影响。

Method: 构建三种检索文档集场景：1) 完全重复的副本、2) LLM改写版本、3) 不同体裁的多样化文档。使用FictionalQA合成虚构问答数据集，确保LLM无法依赖先验知识回答，答案必须来自检索文档。同时控制文档是否包含精确匹配或改写形式的答案。

Result: 重复冗余和LLM改写不会显著提升答案正确性；提供多样化文档可将答案正确性提升17%-47%。进一步分析表明，该提升仅由文档体裁多样性驱动，而非因为生成器可获取到更多相关答案。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了生成器对检索结果多样性的偏好，建议未来检索方法应关注如何为生成器提供多样化文档，而非简单地追求高相关文档的重复或改写，从而更有效地提升RAG生成质量。

Abstract: In RAG, while the retriever typically ranks documents by their individual relevance to the query, the generator instead produces an answer based on the retrieved documents as a whole. This paper investigates how redundancy and diversity from the retrieved document set impact the generator in terms of answer correctness.
  Previous work has provided a mix of findings: some showing that redundancy improves generation by reinforcing relevant information, others that LLM-based paraphrasing of the same content may be beneficial. Many of these studies did not control for confounding factors like whether the documents contained the exact answer or not, and if parametric knowledge plays a role. We conduct a carefully controlled experiment investigating three key scenarios of retrieved document sets: 1) Duplicate (exact copies of the same document), 2) Paraphrased (LLM rephrased versions of one document) and 3) Diverse (documents from different genres each containing relevant information in different forms). We control for which documents contain the answer in exact match or rephrased form.
  Evaluation is done with FictionalQA, a synthetic, fictional question-answer dataset that ensures the LLM generator prior knowledge cannot answer the question; the answer must come from retrieved documents. We show that duplicate redundancy and LLM paraphrasing does not significantly improve answer correctness. However, providing diverse documents is highly beneficial, improving answer correctness by 17%-47%. We further show this improvement is driven by diverse forms of document genre (news, blogs, etc.) alone and not a consequence of more relevant answer being available to generator. Our findings help to direct more attention to how new retrieval methods might improve RAG by catering to the generator preference for diversity in retrieval results.

</details>


### [285] [EchoRec: Multi-Item Prediction-Empowered Generative Recommendation via Cycle-Consistent Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14011)
*Haokai Ma,Aoqi Hu,Yueao Xing,Ruobing Xie,Yonghui Yang,Teng Tu,Lei Meng,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoRec，通过周期一致性整体偏好对齐（cycle-consistent holistic preference alignment）来增强生成式推荐中的多Token预测（MTP）。该方法发现未来行为携带当前行为的语义回声（但随意图转变而衰减），据此设计了两个协同模块：Horizon-aware Preference Generation (HPG) 和 Verifiable Holistic-Preference Alignment (VHA)，在训练时提供密集监督，推理时可完全丢弃辅助组件，几乎不增加在线开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐虽然引入了多Token预测（MTP），但仅利用了其效率优势，未挖掘其作为密集监督信号的潜力。作者通过分析发现，未来行为与当前行为之间存在显著的语义回声（远高于随机对），但这种回声会随意图转变随时间衰减，因此是"信息丰富但顺序相关"的信号。这一发现表明MTP可以作为密集监督，但其顺序敏感性需要被妥善处理。

Method: EchoRec包含两个协同模块：(1) Horizon-aware Preference Generation (HPG)：在基础推荐器上顺序串联轻量级辅助分支，每个分支以其前驱为条件以尊重偏好演化；(2) Verifiable Holistic-Preference Alignment (VHA)：将各分支整合为整体偏好，并通过循环一致投影器（cycle-consistent projectors）将其回映，以抑制虚假对齐，并在可逆传输下提供理论保证排除秩崩溃形式的虚假对齐，使整体偏好保留在解码表示中。所有辅助组件在推理时被丢弃，仅作为训练脚手架。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验证明了EchoRec的优越性，并且该方法自然地获得了多物品生成能力（multi-item generation ability）。

Conclusion: EchoRec成功解锁了MTP作为密集监督的潜力，通过周期一致性对齐机制有效利用未来行为的语义回声，同时通过理论保证确保整体偏好能够保留在解码表示中，最终在不增加推理开销的前提下提升了生成式推荐的性能，并附带获得了多物品生成能力。

Abstract: Generative recommendation autoregressively generates the semantic IDs of the target item, unifying preference modeling and index retrieval within the shared token space. Recent attempts have introduced Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) into this field, yet they primarily inherit its efficiency merit, leaving its potential as dense supervision unexplored. Unlocking this potential hinges on whether future behaviors qualify as informative supervision. Our analysis reveals that future behaviors carry a semantic echo of the current one far above that of random pairs, which nevertheless decays along horizons under intent transitions, making them informative yet order-dependent signals. Motivated by this, we propose EchoRec, which empowers MTP with cycle-consistent holistic preference alignment across multi-horizon for generative recommendation. It comprises two synergistic modules. Horizon-aware Preference Generation (HPG) sequentially chains lightweight auxiliary branches upon the base recommender, where each branch conditions on its predecessor to respect preference evolution. Verifiable Holistic-Preference Alignment (VHA) further consolidates them into the holistic preference and echoes it back through cycle-consistent projectors to suppress spurious alignment, with theoretical guarantees that exclude the rank-collapse form of spurious alignment under an invertible transport, enabling the holistic preference to be retained in the decoding representation. All auxiliary components serve as disposable scaffolding discarded at inference, introducing negligible online serving overhead. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate the superiority of our EchoRec, together with its naturally acquired multi-item generation ability. Our code and datasets will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [286] [HAM-RAG: Hierarchy-Aware Multimodal RAG for Structure-Faithful Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14032)
*Yin Li,Ziyang Hu,Zhiyu Guo,Xiangyu Liu,Wenbin Li,Boo-Ho Yang,Rav Lawana,Ziyue Li,Wei Zeng,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HAM-RAG，一个层级感知的多模态RAG框架，利用文档层级结构作为检索和生成的接地信号，解决现有方法将结构化文档扁平化导致证据选择和放置不忠实的问题。同时引入HAM-Bench基准测试，在多个任务上相比最强非层级基线平均提升17.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG方法将结构化文档扁平化为孤立的文本和图像单元，破坏了文档的原始组织结构和局部文本-图像逻辑关系，导致证据选择和放置不够忠实于源文档。

Method: HAM-RAG将文档层级作为检索和生成阶段的接地信号：在检索阶段利用层级来上下文化文本和视觉证据，在生成阶段保留证据的源位置和局部文本-图像关系到提示中。同时构建了覆盖游戏攻略、网页、科学论文和菜谱文档的HAM-Bench基准。

Result: 在多个骨干模型上，HAM-RAG相比最强非层级基线将多模态平均性能提升17.3%；在Wukong数据集上，Img-CBS指标提升24.2%，展示了显著更好的局部文本-图像对齐能力。

Conclusion: 实验和消融研究共同证明文档层级是忠实图像选择、放置和局部文本-图像对齐的关键接地信号，对技术手册、维护指南和工业SOP等结构化文档的多模态助手具有重要价值。

Abstract: Existing multimodal RAG methods often flatten structured documents into isolated text and image units, weakening the source organization and local text-image logic needed for faithful evidence selection and placement. We propose HAM-RAG, a Hierarchy-Aware Multimodal RAG framework for structure-faithful interleaved generation. HAM-RAG uses document hierarchy as a grounding signal across retrieval and generation, contextualizing textual and visual evidence and preserving source position and local text-image relations in the prompt. We further introduce HAM-Bench, covering Wukong, Wiki, arXiv, and Recipe across game walkthroughs, web pages, scientific papers, and step-wise recipe documents. Across multiple backbones, HAM-RAG improves the main multimodal average by 17.3% over the strongest non-hierarchical baseline. On Wukong, HAM-RAG improves Img-CBS by 24.2% over the strongest non-hierarchical baseline, demonstrating substantially better local text-image alignment. The main experiments and ablation study together demonstrate that document hierarchy is a key grounding signal for faithful image selection, placement, and local text-image alignment. These findings highlight the value of hierarchy-aware grounding for reliable multimodal assistants that generate answers faithful to the source organization, procedural structure, and local text-image evidence of structured documents, such as technical manuals, maintenance guides, and industrial SOPs. The code is available at https://github.com/MCCodeAI/HAM-RAG.git.

</details>


### [287] [TenderKG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14066)
*Yacine Mokhtari,Véra Pukhkoy,Grégory Smits*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TenderKG，一个从2021-2023年法国公共采购数据构建的大规模知识图谱数据集，用于研究在信息稀疏且竞争激烈的环境中进行知识感知推荐，涵盖公司、招标、标段和领域分类等多种实体及其关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共采购是重要的经济活动，但推荐系统在该领域的研究很少，主要是因为缺乏能够捕捉其复杂性的公开数据集。现有的公开数据稀缺阻碍了该领域推荐系统的研究发展。

Method: 从法国公共采购数据（2021-2023年）构建了TenderKG知识图谱数据集，包含公司、招标、标段和工作领域分类等多种异构实体，并通过丰富的语义和结构关系连接。数据集整合了大量侧信息，包括文本描述、层级分类和地理特征。由于只有中标公司可见导致明确的交互信号稀疏，数据集通过丰富的侧信息来弥补这一不足。

Result: 提供了数据集的详细统计和分析，展示了其结构属性、稀疏模式和领域特定特征，为投标人推荐、知识图谱推荐和竞争感知匹配等研究方向提供了基准。

Conclusion: TenderKG为公共采购领域的知识感知推荐研究开辟了新方向，为真实世界高风险的决策场景提供了有价值的评估基准，有望推动投标人推荐、知识图谱推荐和竞争感知匹配等方向的研究。

Abstract: Public procurement represents a major economic activity, where public institutions allocate contracts to companies through competitive tendering processes. Despite its importance, this domain remains underexplored by recommender systems, largely due to the lack of publicly available datasets capturing its complexity. In this paper, we introduce TenderKG, a large-scale knowledge graph dataset constructed from French public procurement data covering the period 2021--2023. The dataset models the procurement ecosystem through heterogeneous entities, including companies, tenders, lots, and domain-specific taxonomies of work domains, connected via rich semantic and structural relations. A key specificity of this setting is that only the awarded companies are visible, resulting in sparse explicit signals of awarded interactions. To overcome this limitation, TenderKG integrates extensive side information on the actors in the French tender market and the tenders, including textual descriptions, hierarchical classifications, and geographical features, enabling the study of knowledge-aware recommendation in a highly constrained and competitive environment. We provide detailed statistics and analyses of the dataset, highlighting its structural properties, sparsity patterns, and domain-specific characteristics. We believe TenderKG opens new research directions in bidder recommendation, knowledge graph-based recommendation, competition-aware matching, and provides a valuable benchmark for evaluating methods in real-world, high-stakes decision-making scenarios.

</details>


### [288] [MACS: A Hybrid Multi-Agent Framework for Reliable Conversational E-Commerce Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14068)
*Juli Huang,Hannah Clay,Sajjad Beygi,Thomas Sarda,Negin Golrezaei,Amin Saberi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MACS（多智能体电商系统），一种混合多智能体框架，用于固定目录场景下的可靠对话式推荐。MACS将语言任务交给LLM，而产品检索、硬约束过滤、品牌排除和渐进松弛等正确性关键操作由商家智能体确定性执行，并配备会话持久化偏好层。在单轮和多轮基准测试中，MACS在通过率、品牌合规性、约束漂移控制等方面均优于纯提示词的目录绑定基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商对话推荐越来越多地由LLM驱动，但实际部署中推荐必须仅从商家固定目录中选取，不能依赖网络搜索或未经证实的产品主张。在此约束下，系统面临可靠性与硬约束的挑战：需满足用户需求、保持库存接地、并在多轮对话中保留用户偏好。现有纯提示词方法在约束保持上表现不佳。

Method: MACS采用混合多智能体架构：LLM负责语言面对任务（理解用户请求、偏好引导、生成响应），而商家智能体以确定性方式执行正确性关键操作（产品检索、硬约束过滤、品牌排除、渐进松弛）。会话持久化偏好层跨轮次追踪约束，支持预算覆盖和排除反转的一致性处理。

Result: 在140查询的单轮基准上，MACS达到最高通过率（87.1%）和完美的品牌合规性（1.000）。在10场景多轮基准上，MACS的宏观Pass@5达72%（优于GPT+Catalog的56%和Gemini+Catalog的52%），且零约束漂移。排除反转场景优势最明显（100% vs 20%/0%），约束累积场景为100% vs 60%/40%。响应质量评分相近（0.751 vs 0.736）。

Conclusion: 混合架构将确定性约束执行与会话持久化偏好追踪相结合，在固定目录商家场景中比纯提示词的目录绑定基线提供更强的可靠性导向性能，为电商对话推荐中的约束满足问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Conversational recommendation for e-commerce is increasingly mediated by large language models (LLMs), yet many real-world deployments operate under a stricter requirement: recommendations must be drawn only from a merchant's fixed catalog, without web search or unsupported product claims. In this setting, the main challenge is reliability under hard constraints: the system must satisfy user requirements, remain grounded in available inventory, and preserve preferences across multiple conversational turns. We present MACS (Multi-Agent Commerce System), a hybrid multi-agent framework for reliable conversational recommendation in fixed-catalog settings. MACS uses LLMs for language-facing tasks such as interpreting user requests, eliciting preferences, and generating responses, while correctness-critical operations, including product retrieval, hard-constraint filtering, brand exclusion, and progressive relaxation, are executed deterministically by the merchant agent. A session-persistent preference layer tracks constraints across turns, enabling consistent handling of budget overwrites and exclusion reversals. On a 140-query single-turn benchmark, MACS achieves the highest pass rate (87.1%) and perfect brand compliance (1.000). On a 10-scenario multi-turn benchmark, MACS achieves the strongest macro Pass@5 (72% vs. 56% GPT+Catalog / 52% Gemini+Catalog) with zero constraint drift. The advantage is sharpest on exclusion reversal (100% vs. 20% / 0%) and constraint accumulation (100% vs. 60% / 40%). Mean judged response quality is similar across systems (0.751 vs. 0.736). These results suggest that hybrid architectures combining deterministic constraint enforcement with session-persistent preference tracking provide stronger reliability-oriented performance than catalog-bound prompt-only baselines in the fixed-catalog merchant setting.

</details>


### [289] [PriCoRec: A Privacy-Aware Cloud-Device Collaborative Framework for Ad Recommendation under Feature Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14429)
*Dairui Liu,Zhongyi Lu,Jitao Lu,Aghiles Salah,Mete Sertkan,Roger Zhe Li,Changhong Jin,Barry Smyth,Xingsheng Guo,Ruihai Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了PriCoRec框架，在云端和端侧协同的推荐系统中将敏感特征保留在设备端，通过云端预排序和端侧精排两阶段分离实现隐私保护，同时保持推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私法规限制了云端处理敏感用户数据（如年龄、性别），阻碍了传统纯云端推荐模型的发展。

Method: 设计云端预排序+端侧排序的协同框架：云端利用非敏感特征进行预排序并加入多样性正则化提升候选质量；设备端利用高度个性化特征进行精排，并通过云端引导的训练机制在保持模型轻量化的同时提升端侧模型性能。

Result: 实验表明该框架在将敏感特征保留在设备端的同时，维持了较强的推荐性能。

Conclusion: 通过云端与端侧的协同分工，能够在隐私合规约束下实现高效且个性化的广告推荐，平衡了隐私保护、推荐质量和设备端资源消耗。

Abstract: Privacy regulations increasingly restrict cloud processing of sensitive user data (e.g., age, gender), hindering traditional cloud-only recommendation models. To mitigate this challenge, we propose a Privacy-aware Collaborative cloud-device ads Recommendation framework (PriCoRec) which personalizes recommendations while keeping sensitive features on-device. While separating recommendation into cloud-based and on-device stages enables privacy-aware deployment, naive splitting suffers from degraded shortlist quality and inefficient on-device inference due to limited private features. We therefore design a collaborative framework that comprises a cloud-based pre-ranking stage using cloud-accessible features, and an on-device ranking stage that locally incorporates highly personalized features. We introduce a diversity regularizer to pre-ranking to improve candidate quality. Moreover, to control device power consumption and computational cost, we incorporate a cloud-guided training mechanism that enhances device model performance while keeping the model lightweight. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework maintains strong recommendation performance while keeping sensitive features on-device.

</details>
